# Audit register, 2026-11-09

This page is the full list of problems found in one audit of UniDTK: 8 slices of the code read in full, plus a live run of the installed build through `udtk`. It says what is wrong and where. It does not propose fixes. Each entry is also a GitHub issue; its number is shown when the issue exists.

## How it was made

- Code: commit `f361292` on `main`. Installed build: files dated 2026-11-06, running.
- Slices: Telegram UI, Telegram backend, Explorer, MP3 player, VPN with micrographics, glyph bar and clock, CLI with build and installer, all stylesheets, the kernel with the contract crate, and the core UI with the live runtime.
- Eight slices were read by subagents on Haiku; about 70 of their findings were re-checked against the source by hand and none was wrong. The rest are marked by status below.
- Status: `reproduced on a live build` means it was triggered or measured in the running app; `confirmed by reading the code` means the lines were read; `not verified at runtime` means it rests on reading alone or on an estimate (for example a contrast ratio computed from the theme tokens).
- Severity: high is data loss, a crash, a security exposure or a check that cannot fail; medium is wrong behaviour users meet; low is cosmetic or latent.

## Telegram

### A001 Telegram backend: config migration deletes the legacy JSON before the replacement is saved

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#19](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/19)) Fixed in `fd18e05`.
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:5949`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:5950`
- Problem: The legacy JSON config is removed before its replacement is written. Both results are discarded. A failed write leaves no stored copy.
- Impact: Accounts, the active account and proxy settings are lost and replaced by defaults with no message to the user.

```text
let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
let _ = save_raw_config(&res);
```

### A002 Telegram backend: receiver thread exits silently on any TDLib receive error

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#20](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/20))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:2434`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:4918`
- Problem: Any receive error or non-UTF-8 reply ends the receiver thread with no log and no state change. The client stays marked as running. Later requests time out after 2 s.
- Impact: The module stops delivering updates and answering requests until the app restarts, and no error is shown.

```text
let text = match td.receive(0.5) {
Ok(None) => continue,
Err(_) => break,
```

### A003 Telegram backend: module shutdown ignores its 1500 ms budget and leaves the receiver running

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#21](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/21))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:6235`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:6236`
- Problem: shutdown() ignores the budget argument and calls only begin_shutdown(). It does not join the receiver thread or destroy the TDLib client.
- Impact: A thread and the TDLib client stay alive after the module reports shutdown.
- Rule: `modules.lifecycle` (docs/reference/modules.md)

```text
fn shutdown(&self, _budget: std::time::Duration) -> Result<(), String> {
begin_shutdown();
```

### A004 Telegram backend: suspend ignores the budget and shuts down the global VPN-linked instance

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#22](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/22))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:6240`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:6243`
- Problem: suspend() takes no budget. It calls shutdown_client on the process-global TELEGRAM_FOR_VPN instead of self.runtime. shutdown_client waits on inner.lock(), which can be held for seconds.
- Impact: Disabling Telegram can exceed the 1500 ms budget and may shut down an instance other than the one the module owns.
- Rule: `modules.lifecycle` (docs/reference/modules.md)

```text
fn suspend(&self) -> Result<(), String> {
SUSPENDED.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);
stop_managed_proxy();
if let Some(inner) = TELEGRAM_FOR_VPN.get() {
shutdown_client(inner);
```

### A005 Telegram backend: suspended module is restarted by any status or chats poll

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#23](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/23))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:2732`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:2917`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:2489`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:2862`
- Problem: SUSPENDED is checked only in sync_vpn_proxy. ensure_started has no suspend check, and status() and chats() call it.
- Impact: Switching the module off does not stop TDLib while anything polls the status or the chat list.
- Rule: `modules.lifecycle` (docs/reference/modules.md)

```text
pub fn status(runtime: &TelegramRuntime) -> TelegramStatus {
if let Err(error) = ensure_started(&runtime.inner) {
```

### A006 Telegram backend: ciadpi adopted from an earlier run is never killed

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#24](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/24))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:157`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:158`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:206`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:111`
- Problem: A listener already answering SOCKS5 on the port is adopted without storing its Child. stop_managed_proxy cannot kill it, and a crash leaves the process running. The probe accepts any process that replies with 05 00.
- Impact: An orphaned ciadpi keeps running after disable, exit or crash, and a foreign SOCKS5 service on the port is used silently.

```text
if managed_proxy_port_open() {
unidtk_api::log(format!(
```

### A007 Telegram: reply-preview cache is keyed by bare message id, shared across chats

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#25](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/25)) Fixed in `fd18e05`.
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:2179`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:2210`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:2217`
- Problem: The reply cache uses only the message id as its key, with no chat id. Telegram message ids are per chat, so a lookup can return a message from a different chat. The has() guard at line 2210 then prevents a refetch.
- Impact: A reply preview can show the text and sender of an unrelated message from another chat. The wrong text stays until the session ends.

```text
|| (telegramRuntime.replyCache?.get(rid));
...
if (!replySnippet && chat?.id && rid && !telegramRuntime.pendingReplies?.has(rid) && !telegramRuntime.replyCache?.has(rid)) {
...
(telegramRuntime.replyCache ||= new Map()).set(rid, msg);
```

### A008 Telegram: failed message edit clears the edit text and drops the edit target

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#26](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/26)) Fixed in `943e803`.
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:4486`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:4494`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:4503`
- Problem: The composer is cleared and the edit target is deleted before the edit request is sent. The catch block only writes to the log, and it does not restore edit mode or the text.
- Impact: If the edit fails, the user loses both the edited text and the edit state, and sees no error.

```text
input.value = "";
...
telegramRuntime.editMessage.delete(chatId);
...
} catch (error) {
  logFrontend("edit message error: " + error);
```

### A009 Telegram: failed send overwrites text the user typed while the send was in flight

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#27](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/27)) Fixed in `943e803`.
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:4540`
- Problem: The input is cleared when a send starts. On failure, the original text is written back with an assignment that replaces the whole field value. Any text typed during the send is lost.
- Impact: Text the user typed while a send was pending is overwritten when the send fails.

```text
if (input) {
  input.value = text;
```

### A010 Telegram: removing an account runs at once with no confirmation or Undo

- Severity: high. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#28](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/28))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:2857`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:2862`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:5186`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:5191`
- Problem: One click on the remove control removes the account and clears all per-chat state. The settings-panel copy of the control behaves the same way. Failures are only written to console.error.
- Impact: An account can be removed by one misclick and cannot be restored from the widget. The user gets no visible error if removal fails.

```text
body.querySelectorAll(".tg-account-remove-btn").forEach((btn) => {
btn.addEventListener("click", async (e) => {
surfaceManager.closeSurface(dropdown);
clearTelegramAccountState();
```

### A011 Telegram: deleting a message removes it for everyone with no confirmation or Undo

- Severity: high. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#29](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/29))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:4236`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:4240`
- Problem: The delete action always sends revoke: true, so the message is removed for every participant. It runs on one click with no confirmation and no Undo. Errors are written only to the frontend log.
- Impact: A message can be deleted for all chat members by one click and cannot be restored. A failed deletion shows no message to the user.

```text
onClick: async () => {
  if (!chatId || !msgId) return;
  try {
    await invoke("telegram_delete_messages", { request: { chatId, messageIds: [msgId], revoke: true } });
```

### A012 Telegram: clearing chat history is irreversible and runs with no confirmation

- Severity: high. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#30](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/30))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:4300`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:4304`
- Problem: Clear history sends revoke: true, which deletes the history for all participants. It runs from a context-menu click with no confirmation and no Undo. Errors are written only to the frontend log.
- Impact: The full chat history can be removed for everyone by one click and cannot be restored. A failed clear shows no message to the user.

```text
await invoke("telegram_delete_chat_history", { request: { chatId: chatId, removeFromChatList: false, revoke: true } });
```

### A013 Telegram: deleting a chat is irreversible and runs with no confirmation or Undo

- Severity: high. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#31](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/31))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:4313`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:4320`
- Problem: Delete chat sends revoke: true with removeFromChatList: true. It runs from a context-menu click with no confirmation and no Undo. Errors are written only to the frontend log.
- Impact: The chat is removed for all participants by one click and cannot be restored. A failed delete shows no message to the user.

```text
await invoke("telegram_delete_chat_history", { request: { chatId: chatId, removeFromChatList: true, revoke: true } });
```

### A014 Telegram backend: proxy sync holds the global inner mutex across blocking proxy start

- Severity: high. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#32](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/32))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:2870`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:2899`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:2453`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:1776`
- Problem: sync_vpn_proxy holds the single TelegramInner mutex while apply_proxy_config may spawn ciadpi and wait up to 5 s. The receiver thread takes the same mutex for every TDLib update. The status poll also runs a socket probe under the lock while the VPN is down.
- Impact: All TDLib updates and commands stall for up to about 5 s per call.

```text
let mut g = inner.lock().unwrap();
if let Err(error) = g.apply_proxy_config(&config) {
```

### A015 Telegram backend: presence is not merged once the chat list is exhausted

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#33](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/33))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:2930`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:2980`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:5046`
- Problem: After chats_exhausted is set, chats() returns before the status merge. parse_chat always sets status to None.
- Impact: User presence never appears in the chat list after the first complete load.

```text
return sorted_chats(g.chats.values().cloned().collect());
```

### A016 Telegram backend: forum subchat fallback matches all chats for a title starting with |

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#34](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/34))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:4011`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:3987`
- Problem: An empty prefix before the first bar yields starts_with(""), which is true for every chat. Any getForumTopics error also falls through to the heuristic with no error. docs/modules/telegram.md Step 7 describes the pattern but not the empty-prefix case.
- Impact: The topic column can list the whole chat list as subchats of an unrelated parent.

```text
(parent_title.contains('|') && c.title.starts_with(parent_title.split('|').next().unwrap_or("").trim()))
```

### A017 Telegram backend: hardcoded @extra 1001 collides with the sequential request counter

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#35](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/35)) Fixed in `894f1d9`.
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:1490`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:1460`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:2401`
- Problem: Fire-and-forget getMe and getUser use the literal @extra 1001. alloc_extra counts up from 1, so after about 1000 requests a reply with that id is read as the account's own user id.
- Impact: The stored account identity and name can be overwritten with another user's data.

```text
if extra == Some(1001) {
```

### A018 Telegram backend: failed pin lookups with not found are reported as no pin

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#36](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/36))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:3374`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:3353`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:3367`
- Problem: The function matches by substring, while its own doc comment says to match only the leading code. Any error containing that phrase returns Ok(None).
- Impact: A pin bar is hidden even when the lookup actually failed.

```text
err.to_ascii_lowercase().contains("not found")
```

### A019 Telegram backend: message edit keeps stale entities in the cache

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#37](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/37))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:3691`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:3690`
- Problem: edit_message updates only the text field of the cached message. Entities from the old text stay attached.
- Impact: Formatting and mention highlights are drawn at old offsets after an edit.

```text
if let Some(m) = msgs.iter_mut().find(|m| m.id == request.message_id) {
m.text = text.to_string();
```

### A020 Telegram backend: account removal deletes the active TDLib directory before shutdown

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#38](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/38))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:4826`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:4837`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:4825`
- Problem: The data directory is removed before the client is shut down, and the delete error is discarded. The default account directory is never removed.
- Impact: Deletion can fail partway while files are open, and the default account's session stays on disk after removal.

```text
if account_id != "default" && data_dir.exists() {
let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&data_dir);
```

### A021 Telegram backend: config save and proxy disable errors are discarded

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#39](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/39))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:1733`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:1048`
- Problem: The config save result is ignored after account metadata updates. The proxy-disable result is ignored on the fallback path.
- Impact: A failed save or a failed proxy disable is invisible, so stored and effective state can differ.

```text
let _ = save_raw_config(&config);
```

- Problem: switch_account and add_account discard the start error. The hint "Switching account..." remains until a later status poll.
- Impact: After a failed start, the UI shows a stale hint with no reason.

```text
let _ = ensure_started(&runtime.inner);
```

```text
inner,
```

### A024 Telegram backend: lock poisoning turns every later command into a panic

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#42](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/42))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:2453`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:2463`
- Problem: 95 of the 99 unwrap or expect calls are Mutex lock unwraps. After a panic under the lock, commands panic while the receiver silently drops all updates. The four non-lock unwraps are guarded or in tests.
- Impact: One panic disables the module until the app restarts.

```text
if let Ok(mut locked) = inner.lock() {
locked.handle_update(value);
```

### A025 Telegram backend: 64-bit chat order and thread ids are serialized as JSON numbers

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#43](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/43))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:261`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:349`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:1205`
- Problem: order, message_thread_id and threadId are i64 values sent to JavaScript as JSON numbers. The magnitudes of real TDLib values were not verified.
- Impact: Values above 2^53 would lose precision in JavaScript.

```text
pub order: i64,
```

### A026 Telegram backend: omitted revoke flag defaults to deleting for all participants

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#44](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/44))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:410`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:454`
- Problem: When the revoke field is omitted, message deletion and history deletion revoke for all participants.
- Impact: A request without the field deletes messages for other users too.

```text
#[serde(default = "default_true")]
pub revoke: bool,
```

### A028 Telegram: reply target is cleared before send and not restored on failure

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#46](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/46)) Fixed in `943e803`.
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:4517`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:4637`
- Problem: The reply target is deleted before the send request runs. The failure path of the text send and of the attachment send does not restore it.

### A029 Telegram: cancelling an edit keeps the edit text as draft, which returns on render

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#47](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/47))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:4607`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:4679`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:4680`
- Problem: Cancelling an edit clears the input without updating telegramRuntime.drafts. The edit text was saved there earlier by the input listener. The next full render restores that draft when the input is not focused.
- Impact: A cancelled edit reappears in the composer after a re-render.

```text
if (input) input.value = "";
...
const draft = telegramRuntime.drafts.get(active.id);
if (draft) composerInput.value = draft;
```

### A030 Telegram: deleting a chat leaves it in the archive list

- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:4315`
- Problem: The delete-chat handler filters only telegramRuntime.chats. The archivedChats list is not updated.
- Impact: A chat deleted from the archive view stays listed there.

```text
if (Array.isArray(telegramRuntime.chats)) {
  telegramRuntime.chats = telegramRuntime.chats.filter((c) => c.id !== chatId);
```

- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:1315`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:5388`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:5464`
- Problem: The IIFE always returns true, so the opened === false branches can never run. The same dead check appears at lines 5388 and 5464.
- Impact: The "Не удалось открыть файл" message can never be shown, even when opening fails.

```text
  try {
    await invoke("open_explorer", { path: path });

  }
  return true;

if (opened === false && chat?.id) {
```

### A032 Telegram: openPath rejection escapes the click handler with no user feedback

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#50](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/50))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:1311`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:5387`
- Problem: If openPath rejects, the rejection is not caught and propagates out of the click handler as an unhandled promise rejection. The same pattern is copied at line 5387.
- Impact: A failed file open gives the user no feedback and leaves an unhandled rejection in the console.

```text
} catch {
  await openPath(path);
}
```

### A033 Telegram: backend availability check is always true because invoke returns a Promise

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#51](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/51))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:5840`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:5875`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:5804`, `core/ui/unidtk-bridge.js:504`
- Problem: invoke is an async function, so the expression always yields a truthy Promise. The backend_unavailable state and the disabled save button are therefore never reached. The call also starts an IPC request during render with no catch.
- Impact: The setup form never shows its backend-unavailable state, and a failing status request during render is unhandled.

```text
const backendAvailable = !!invoke("telegram_status");
const state = backendAvailable ? (status?.authState || "mock") : "backend_unavailable";
```

### A034 Telegram: older-history failure has no cooldown, so each scroll re-requests

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#52](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/52))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:5555`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:5556`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:3343`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:3345`
- Problem: The catch branch of loadTelegramOlderMessages sets no cooldown, unlike the empty-result branches that set a 60 s cooldown. The scroll handler calls the loader on every scroll event above the top threshold. The error is swallowed.
- Impact: While the backend fails, every scroll event near the top re-invokes telegram_older_messages with no visible error.

```text
} catch {
  // Older history is best-effort; leave the current thread intact on failure.
}
...
if (messageScroller.scrollTop > 100 || !chatId) return;
```

### A035 Telegram: failed mark-read is never retried for the same newest message

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#53](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/53))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:684`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:685`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:689`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:690`
- Problem: The newest id is recorded as read before telegram_mark_read is sent. Its failure is swallowed, so the dedupe check at line 684 prevents any retry for that id.
- Impact: A failed read receipt is not sent again for the same message, and the failure is not reported.

```text
if (!force && newestId && lastMarkedId === newestId) return;
if (newestId) telegramRuntime.lastMarkedRead.set(chatId, newestId);
...
const p = invoke("telegram_mark_read", { chatId: chatId });
if (p && typeof p.then === "function") p.catch(() => {});
```

```text
}).catch(() => {
  if (generation === telegramRuntime.accountGeneration) telegramRuntime.forumTopics.set(chatId, []);
...
if (telegramRuntime.forumTopics.has(chatId)) return;
```

```text
...
```

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#56](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/56))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:5104`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:5105`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:5109`
- Problem: The optional chain returns undefined without throwing when navigator.clipboard is missing, so the success label is shown. The file's copyTelegramText function instead uses the backend clipboard command, and this path does not.

### A043 Telegram backend: chat list load failure returns a partial list with no error

- Severity: medium. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#61](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/61))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:2959`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:2915`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:2926`
- Problem: Non-404 TDLib errors only log and return the partial list. chats() returns a Vec with no error channel. The unauthorized state returns an empty list silently.

```text
unidtk_api::log(format!("telegram: load chats: {error}"));
break;
```

### A044 Telegram backend: login errors reach the UI as raw TDLib codes and text

- Problem: Each auth error is returned as Ok with the raw CODE: MESSAGE text as the hint. There is no flood-wait time and no resend-code or change-phone path. The file contains no resendAuthenticationCode call.
- Impact: The login flow dead-ends when a code expires or a flood wait applies.

```text
let mut g = inner.lock().unwrap();
g.auth_hint = error;
Ok(g.status())
```

### A061 Telegram backend: second init leaks the previous runtime and routes VPN events to the first

- Severity: medium. Type: architecture. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#79](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/79))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:6251`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:6224`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:6231`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:584`
- Problem: resume ignores its context and does not call init. init overwrites self.runtime without shutting down the old client. TELEGRAM_FOR_VPN is a OnceLock set once, so VPN events keep reaching the first instance. The current kernel calls init once, so this path is latent.
- Impact: After a second init, the old TDLib client and ciadpi keep running, and VPN changes do not reach the live runtime.
- Rule: `modules.lifecycle` (docs/reference/modules.md)

```text
let _ = TELEGRAM_FOR_VPN.set(Arc::clone(&inner));
```

- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:5490`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:1839`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:4142`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:1726`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:1824`
- Problem: Chat previews, attachment labels, login hints and fallback names are English literals with no translation path. The TDLib system language is fixed to en.
- Impact: Localizing the module requires code changes in many places.

```text
"messageVoiceNote" => "Voice message".into(),
"messageVideoNote" => "Video message".into(),
```

### A069 Telegram backend: TDLib API credentials ship as source constants and are saved

- Severity: medium. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#87](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/87))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:58`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:59`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:5973`
- Problem: The TDLib api_id and api_hash are defined as constants in the source (DEFAULT_TELEGRAM_API_ID and DEFAULT_TELEGRAM_API_HASH, values <redacted>). with_config_defaults writes them into the config, and save_config persists them.
- Impact: The application credentials are public in the source and stored in the saved config.

```text
config.api_hash = DEFAULT_TELEGRAM_API_HASH.to_string();
```

### A070 Telegram backend: directory layout names are hardcoded outside unidtk_api::paths

- Severity: medium. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#88](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/88))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:5908`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:5912`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:5917`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:1818`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:6017`
- Problem: The module hardcodes the directory names tdlib, accounts, telegram_config.json, db and files. bundled_tdjson_path hardcodes the tdlib-native layout segments.
- Impact: A change to the install layout requires editing the Telegram module.

```text
let base = unidtk_api::paths::data_dir().join("tdlib");
```

### A071 Telegram backend: build machine source path is compiled into the binary

- Severity: medium. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#89](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/89))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:6051`
- Problem: CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR is baked in at compile time and used as a candidate tdjson.dll location.
- Impact: The fallback path points to the developer's checkout on every machine that runs the binary.
- Rule: `install.no-absolute-paths` (docs/reference/install.md)

```text
std::path::PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))
```

- Impact: Port conflicts and network-specific failures cannot be tuned without a rebuild.

```text
"--disorder", "3+s",
```

### A074 Telegram: literal millisecond timers and an ease-out string bypass motion tokens

- Severity: medium. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#92](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/92))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:3108`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:1118`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:5108`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:5124`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:3477`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:4428`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:7118`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:7022`
- Problem: Timers use literal millisecond values (1400, 2500, 1500, 4000, 250, 200, 300). The collapse fallback uses 320, and an animation uses the literal easing 'ease-out'.
- Impact: These timings and easings cannot follow the shared motion tokens, so they stay out of step with the rest of the UI.
- Rule: `motion.no-hardcoded-ms` (docs/reference/motion.md)

```text
setTimeout(() => target.classList.remove("tg-highlight"), 1400);
...
}, 2500);
...
 const dur = typeof getDuration === "function" ? getDuration("motion") : 320;
...
badge.animate?.({ opacity: [0, 1] }, { duration: getDuration('hover'), easing: 'ease-out' });
```

### A075 Telegram: logout button and dimmed account panel are styled inline in JavaScript

- Severity: medium. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#93](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/93))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:5868`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:5910`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:2837`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:5167`
- Problem: Appearance is set inline in JavaScript, with literal alpha values and a literal opacity of 0.4, rather than through CSS tokens or classes. The logout style is repeated at line 5910.
- Impact: Visual changes to logout or to the dimmed state must be made in JavaScript, outside the theme tokens.
- Rule: `theme.tokens-only` (docs/reference/desk.md)

```text
<button type="button" class="tg-auth-action tg-auth-logout" style="background: rgba(var(--status-error-rgb), 0.2); color: var(--status-error); border-color: rgba(var(--status-error-rgb), 0.3);">
...
if (bodyEl) bodyEl.style.opacity = "0.4";
```

### A076 Telegram backend: chat avatars download automatically with no user setting

- Severity: medium. Type: customization. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#94](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/94))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:1921`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:2388`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:4590`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:25`
- Problem: updateNewChat queues a download for each chat photo at priority 1. Download priority, media auto-download and the message cache size are constants, not settings.
- Impact: The user cannot limit network and disk use caused by media downloads.

```text
"priority": 1,
```

### A077 Telegram: page sizes, cache limits and dock/expanded sizes are not configurable

- Severity: medium. Type: customization. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#95](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/95))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:150`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:152`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:153`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:154`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:4802`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:7141`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:5519`, `modules/telegram/ui/metadata.json:21`
- Problem: Chat page size, rail chat count, cache limits, default dock size and the expanded width clamp are fixed in code. History cannot be loaded beyond 300 messages in the widget. metadata.json exposes only the avatarColor look option.
- Impact: Users cannot adjust list density, widget size or how much history is kept in memory.
- Rule: `modules.metadata` (docs/reference/modules.md)

```text
const TELEGRAM_CHAT_PAGE_SIZE = 24;
const TELEGRAM_RAIL_CHAT_LIMIT = 14;
const TELEGRAM_MAX_CACHED_CHATS = 6;
const TELEGRAM_MAX_CACHED_MESSAGES = 300;
const targetW = Math.min(560, Math.max(440, canvasW - margin * 2 - 40));
```

- Problem: Opening a chat marks it read and sends telegram_mark_read automatically. No setting turns this off.

### A079 Telegram backend: TDLib database is created with an empty encryption key

- Severity: medium. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#97](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/97))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:1828`
- Problem: setTdlibParameters sends an empty database encryption key. The session data in the TDLib database is stored unencrypted.
- Impact: Anyone with read access to the user's local app data can read the Telegram session.

```text
"database_encryption_key": ""
```

### A080 Telegram backend: saved config holds the API hash and proxy password in cleartext

- Severity: medium. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#98](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/98))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:4686`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:510`
- Problem: save_config serializes the API hash and proxy password without encryption. The file is written with a truncating write, so an interrupted write can leave it incomplete.
- Impact: Proxy credentials and the API hash are readable from the local config file.

```text
std::fs::write(path, text).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
```

- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:1518`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:2786`
- Problem: The enabled-proxy log line includes the endpoint key, and the key contains the proxy username.
- Impact: Proxy credential identity is written to the journal.

```text
"telegram: proxy enabled id={id} {key} (is_enabled={is_enabled})"
format!("{kind}://{username}@{server}:{port}")
```

- Problem: load_config runs in status(), chats() and sync_vpn_proxy. update_account_with_user writes the config on each updateUser and user reply. with_config_defaults probes the file system on every load.

### A085 Telegram: every full render refetches the active chat history over IPC

- Severity: medium. Type: performance. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#103](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/103))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:5227`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:2705`
- Problem: renderTelegramWidget force-refetches the active chat's message window on every call. Full renders are triggered from many paths, including status and connection updates.
- Impact: Each render repeats the telegram_messages IPC call and its JSON and DOM work for the whole window.

```text
if (expanded && active?.id) refreshTelegramMessages(card, widgetId, active.id, true);
```

### A086 Telegram backend: trimmed outgoing text is sent with untrimmed entity offsets

- Severity: low. Type: bug. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#104](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/104))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:3563`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:3622`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:3572`
- Problem: send_message trims the text but passes the frontend entity offsets unchanged. The offsets are computed by the frontend on the untrimmed string, which is UNVERIFIED.
- Impact: Leading whitespace shifts mention entities onto the wrong characters.

```text
let text = request.text.trim();
"text": text,
```

### A088 Telegram backend: status event is sent to Tauri, bypassing the contract emitter

- Severity: low. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#106](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/106))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:4750`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:4808`, `core/api/src/lib.rs:202`
- Problem: The status event is sent straight to Tauri. unidtk_api::emit is the path the module's other events use to reach both doors.
- Impact: telegram-status-update does not reach WebSocket clients.
- Rule: `modules.contract-only` (docs/reference/modules.md)

```text
let _ = tauri::Emitter::emit(&app, "telegram-status-update", &status);
```

### A089 Telegram backend: TdJson load leaks the library handle on symbol or client failure

- Severity: low. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#107](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/107))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:4888`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:4894`
- Problem: After LoadLibraryExW succeeds, the symbol lookups and the null-client check return early without FreeLibrary.
- Impact: Each failed attempt leaks one library handle during the retry loop.

```text
let create_fn: TdCreate = load_symbol(library, b"td_json_client_create\0")?;
return Err("td_json_client_create returned null".into());
```

### A090 Telegram backend: TdJson is declared Sync without an established thread-safety basis

- Severity: low. Type: bug. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#108](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/108))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:4868`
- Problem: send runs on command threads while receive runs on the receiver thread. The thread-safety of td_json_client_* is not established in this file.
- Impact: If TDLib is not safe for this use, the FFI calls could race.

```text
unsafe impl Sync for TdJson {}
```

### A091 Telegram backend: SHUTTING_DOWN latch is process-global and never reset

- Severity: low. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#109](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/109))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:2746`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:2750`
- Problem: The latch is set in begin_shutdown and never cleared, and that call also kills the managed proxy. The only caller found is the exit path.
- Impact: Any future non-exit caller would leave the proxy disabled until restart.

```text
static SHUTTING_DOWN: std::sync::atomic::AtomicBool = std::sync::atomic::AtomicBool::new(false);
```

### A092 Telegram backend: malformed ids and parse failures turn into silent empty values

- Severity: low. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#110](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/110))
- Where: `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:5896`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:3466`
- Problem: parse_chat_id returns 0 for malformed ids, and 0 is sent to TDLib. messages_around turns a parse failure into an empty window.
- Impact: The user sees an opaque TDLib error or an empty history instead of an input error.

```text
chat_id.parse::<i64>().unwrap_or_default()
```

- Severity: low. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#111](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/111))

- Problem: The context-menu reaction sends no reactionType and no remove flag. The reaction pill path sends remove: isChosen.

### A094 Telegram: Enter during IME composition sends the half-typed text

- Severity: low. Type: bug. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#112](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/112))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:4744`
- Problem: The Enter handler does not check e.isComposing, and the file contains no isComposing check. Enter that commits an IME composition therefore sends the message. Impact on the user's input layout was not verified.

```text
if (e.key === "Enter" && !e.shiftKey) {
  e.preventDefault();
```

### A099 Telegram: dead and tautological expressions and a literal widget id fallback

- Severity: low. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#117](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/117))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:78`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:709`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:1603`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:1997`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:3015`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:6174`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:6143`
- Problem: Several expressions are tautological: both ternary branches are equal, null || null is repeated, and convertFileSrc is checked for truthiness though it is always imported. The empty catch at 6143 hides failures. Several places fall back to the literal widget id "telegram".

### A102 Telegram: layout thresholds and SVG ring constants are literal magic numbers

- Severity: low. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#120](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/120))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:1374`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:1383`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:3331`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:4682`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:1141`, `modules/telegram/ui/widget.js:6466`
- Problem: Pixel and count thresholds (36, 120, 100, 140, 250, 24, 160, 25, 120) are literal numbers. The ring circumference in JavaScript and the radius in the SVG markup are duplicated, and can drift apart.
- Impact: Layout behaviour is tuned in several places with no shared source, and the ring geometry can fall out of step between the script and the markup.

```text
const atLatest = scroller.scrollHeight - scroller.scrollTop - scroller.clientHeight <= 36;
if (messageRows.length > 120) {
const COMPOSER_MAX_H = 120;
const circumference = 524.65; // 2 * pi * 83.5
```

## Explorer

### A110 Explorer: This PC context menu offers Delete and Cut on drive roots and shortcut places

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#128](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/128)) Fixed in `65dd0bb`.
- Where: `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.js:963`, `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.js:971`, `modules/explorer/ui/context-menu.js:143`, `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.js:2407`
- Problem: This PC drive and place items are passed to the folder context menu with is_dir set, so Copy, Cut, Delete and Rename are offered on them. Delete then calls delete_to_recycle_bin on the drive root or place without a confirmation. Paste of a drive root copies the whole volume.
- Impact: A right-click on a drive root or a shortcut folder can send it to the Recycle Bin or copy an entire drive.

```text
el.addEventListener('click', (e) => this.selectFile({ ...item, is_dir: true }, e));
this.contextMenu.show(e.clientX, e.clientY, 'file', item);
```

### A111 Explorer: Silent delete flags may remove items permanently on non-Recycle-Bin volumes

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#129](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/129))
- Where: `modules/explorer/rust/files.rs:329`
- Problem: Delete sets the no-confirmation and silent flags together with the undo flag. On volumes without a Recycle Bin the shell may remove items permanently without a prompt. Not run on such a volume.
- Impact: Items on network or removable volumes could be lost with no prompt and no Undo offered.

```text
fFlags: (FOF_ALLOWUNDO.0 | FOF_NOCONFIRMATION.0 | FOF_SILENT.0) as u16,
```

### A113 Explorer: History push appends after forward entries, so Back skips a folder

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#131](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/131)) Fixed in `65dd0bb`.
- Where: `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.js:349`, `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.js:2215`
- Problem: navigateTo appends the new path at the end of history instead of after the current index, so forward entries stay in the stack.
- Impact: After Back and a new navigation, the next Back goes to a later folder instead of the previous one.

```text
tab.history.push(path);
tab.historyIndex = tab.history.length - 1;
```

### A114 Explorer: A slow drive query caches raw drive letters for the whole session

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#132](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/132)) Fixed in `65dd0bb`.
- Where: `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.js:131`, `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.js:139`, `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.js:118`
- Problem: After a timed-out drive query the raw-letter list is cached. getDrives returns that cache on every later call, and the cache is never invalidated.
- Impact: Drive labels and capacities are missing for the session, and drives connected later do not appear until restart.

```text
const raw = await this.invokeWithTimeout('get_raw_drives', {}, 1000);
this._cachedDrives = drives;
```

### A115 Explorer: Delete, copy, paste, Backspace and F5 handlers stay active on the desktop after the explorer is closed

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: reproduced on a live build. ([#133](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/133)) Fixed in `65dd0bb`.
- Where: `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.js:2034`, `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.js:1986`, `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:2348`
- Problem: The Enter handler checks that the explorer overlay is visible; this second handler does not. closeExplorer only removes the `visible` class and keeps the selection. With stubbed handlers on the live build, all five hotkeys fired while the explorer was hidden.
- Impact: Pressing Delete on the desktop sends the hidden explorer's selected files to the Recycle Bin with no prompt; Ctrl+V pastes into the hidden tab's folder.

```text
document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
  if (e.target.tagName === 'INPUT' || e.target.tagName === 'TEXTAREA') return;
  if (e.key === 'Delete') this.deleteSelected();
```

### A117 Explorer: Terminal command embeds folder path with only ASCII single quotes escaped

- Severity: high. Type: security. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#135](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/135))
- Where: `modules/explorer/rust/commands.rs:247`, `modules/explorer/rust/commands.rs:251`
- Problem: The folder path is embedded in a PowerShell command string, and only ASCII single quotes are escaped. Other single-quote characters in folder names can change the executed command. Analysis only, not executed.
- Impact: A folder with an untrusted name could run extra PowerShell commands when the user opens a terminal in it.

```text
.args(["-NoExit", "-Command", &format!("Set-Location -LiteralPath '{}'", target_str.replace('\'', "''"))])
```

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#141](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/141))

- Problem: The breadcrumb builder splits the path on separators and rejoins the segments with single backslashes, so the leading backslashes of a UNC path are lost.
- Impact: Clicking a UNC breadcrumb navigates to a path that is not valid.

```text
const parts = path.split(/[\\/]/).filter(Boolean);
cumPath += (i === 0 ? '' : (cumPath.endsWith('\\') ? '' : '\\')) + part;
```

```text
const parts = tab.path.split(/[\\/]/).filter(Boolean);
```


### A128 Explorer: New Folder never gets a number and retries silently on any error

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#146](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/146))


### A130 Explorer: Failed search index build is never retried; This PC reuses an old index

### A137 Explorer: Archive extraction silently overwrites existing destination files

- Where: `modules/explorer/rust/archive.rs:108`

### A138 Explorer: Create archive overwrites its target and stores only top-level names

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#156](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/156))
- Where: `modules/explorer/rust/archive.rs:162`, `modules/explorer/rust/archive.rs:166`
- Problem: create_archive truncates an existing target archive without a check. Each input contributes only its top-level name, and a folder becomes one empty directory entry.
- Impact: Creating an archive over an existing one destroys it, and entries with the same name collide.

```text
let file = File::create(archive_path).map_err(|e| format!("Failed to create archive: {}", e))?;
```

### A139 Explorer: block_on is called inside the socket IPC handler

- Where: `modules/explorer/rust/mod.rs:30`
- Problem: The socket handler calls block_on for get_drives. Whether the caller is a Tokio runtime thread was not verified, because core/kernel/ipc.rs was not read.
- Impact: If the caller is a runtime thread the call panics. Otherwise the IPC thread blocks on drive enumeration.

### A145 Explorer: Module Cargo.toml depends on tauri and tokio beyond unidtk-api

- Severity: medium. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#163](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/163))
- Where: `modules/explorer/rust/Cargo.toml:27`, `modules/explorer/rust/Cargo.toml:28`, `modules/explorer/rust/commands.rs:75`
- Problem: The module declares tauri and tokio as direct dependencies, and commands.rs uses tauri::async_runtime. The module contract is unidtk-api only.
- Impact: The module is coupled to the Tauri framework and bypasses the contract crate.
- Rule: `modules.contract-only` (docs/reference/modules.md)

```text
tokio = { version = "1", features = ["rt", "macros", "sync"] }
tauri = { version = "2" }
```

### A146 Explorer: Hardcoded C: paths for Recycle Bin and home fallback

- Problem: The Recycle Bin sidebar entry is hardcoded to C:\$Recycle.Bin, which is the per-volume storage root listing per-user SID folders. window.rs uses SystemDrive for the same entry, and the home fallback is a literal C:\.

### A152 Explorer: Animation and timer durations are literal milliseconds in JavaScript

### A153 Explorer: Limits, caps and timeouts are hardcoded in JS and Rust

- Severity: medium. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#171](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/171))
- Where: `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.js:10`, `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.js:95`, `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.js:1919`, `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.js:2380`, `modules/explorer/rust/commands.rs:12`, `modules/explorer/rust/search.rs:6`, `modules/explorer/rust/fs.rs:133`, `modules/explorer/rust/archive.rs:76`
- Problem: Magic numbers and limits are hardcoded: render batch sizes of 24, 48 and 48; drive timeouts of 1500 ms and 1000 ms; 100 retries; 30 displayed results; 100 search results; a 20,000-entry index cap at depth 4; a folder-size walk limit of depth 32 and 50,000 entries; a 4 GiB extraction cap; a 1 MiB text preview cap; clipboard retries of 10 attempts and 20 ms; raw drive-type numbers; and a 1024-character buffer. The folder-size walk stops at its limits without any signal.
- Impact: Limits cannot be changed without editing code, and folder sizes can be partial with no indication.

```text
const APPEAR_LIMIT = 24;
```

### A159 Explorer: Single-entry extraction lacks containment check and size budget

- Severity: medium. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#177](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/177))
- Where: `modules/explorer/rust/archive.rs:141`, `modules/explorer/rust/archive.rs:155`
- Problem: extract_single falls back to the mangled entry name and has no containment check. It copies the entry with no byte limit. Reserved device names such as CON or NUL are not validated.
- Impact: Single-entry extraction has neither a size cap nor the containment guard used by full extraction.

```text
let safe_name = entry
.enclosed_name()
.map(|p| p.to_path_buf())
.unwrap_or_else(|| std::path::PathBuf::from(entry.mangled_name()));
```

### A163 Explorer: Search mutex unwrap panics on poison; equal-score results have no tie-break

- Severity: low. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#181](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/181))
- Where: `modules/explorer/rust/commands.rs:292`, `modules/explorer/rust/search.rs:209`
- Problem: Every search command unwraps the mutex, so a poisoned lock panics the later commands. Equal-score results have no tie-break, so the 100-result cut varies between queries.
- Impact: One panic in a search command breaks all later search commands, and result order varies between identical queries.

```text
results.sort_by(|a, b| {
b.score
.partial_cmp(&a.score)
.unwrap_or(std::cmp::Ordering::Equal)
});
```

- Severity: low. Type: documentation. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#196](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/196))
- Where: `docs/modules/explorer.md:274`, `docs/modules/explorer.md:277`, `docs/modules/explorer.md:93`, `docs/modules/explorer.md:189`, `docs/modules/explorer.md:283`
- Problem: The chapter says names are inserted unescaped and that escapeHtml is used only for search, but the code escapes names in grid and list rows. It also describes a missing previewPanel.showProperties method, says raw times are seconds, and gives wrong line numbers.
- Impact: Maintainers reading the chapter get a wrong security picture and wrong code locations.

```text
- **Names are inserted into markup without escaping.** `renderGrid` and `renderList` put `item.name` into `innerHTML` and `aria-label`
```

## MP3 player

### A179 MP3 player: AI DJ button shows on after restart while backend stays off

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#197](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/197))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/ui/widget.js:285`, `modules/mp3-player/ui/widget.js:1587`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/library/config.rs:23`
- Problem: The AI DJ mode is saved only in UI config. On startup the widget highlights it, but no code calls toggle_ai_dj, and the backend default is disabled. The config field ai_dj_enabled is parsed and written but never read.
- Impact: The mode button and the backend disagree after every restart.

```text
if (aiDj) activeMode = "ai_dj";
else if (autoShuffle) activeMode = "shuffle";
```

### A180 MP3 player: failed toggle_ai_dj call is swallowed while the mode is saved as on

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#198](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/198))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/ui/widget.js:529`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/playback/player.rs:131`
- Problem: The result of the toggle_ai_dj call is discarded, so an enable failure is not shown. The mode is saved to config before the call's outcome is known.
- Impact: When enabling fails, the button stays in AI DJ mode with no error shown.

```text
invoke("toggle_ai_dj", { enabled: true }).catch(() => {});
```

### A181 MP3 player: sync failure reason appears only in aria-label, row shows bare status

- Severity: high. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#199](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/199))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/ui/widget.js:1777`, `modules/mp3-player/ui/widget.js:1778`
- Problem: The visible row shows the label, status and progress only. The task message, which carries the yt-dlp reason, is set as aria-label and is not displayed.
- Impact: Users see a failed sync as an unexplained status word.

```text
row.textContent = syncTaskLabel(task);
if (task.message || task.current) row.setAttribute("aria-label", task.message || task.current);
```

### A182 MP3 player: suspend and resume skip the lifecycle contract; EOF monitor thread never exits

- Severity: high. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#200](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/200))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/rust/commands.rs:227`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/commands.rs:236`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/playback/player.rs:111`, `core/kernel/main.rs:198`
- Problem: suspend only sets a flag, pauses playback and stops the visualizer. It does not call shutdown and does not stop analysis, EOF monitoring or sync. resume ignores its context argument and reuses the old state and store. The EOF monitor loop exits only when its PlayerManager clone is the last one, and the kernel keeps a managed AppState copy, so the thread does not exit and each init starts another monitor.
- Impact: Each switch leaves a monitor thread and background work running, so resource use grows with every toggle.
- Rule: `modules.lifecycle` (docs/reference/modules.md)

```text
fn suspend(&self) -> Result<(), String> {
SUSPENDED.store(true, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::SeqCst);
if let Some(state) = self.state() {
state.set_visualizer_enabled(false)?;
```

### A183 MP3 player: missing libmpv-2.dll stops the whole process from starting

- Severity: high. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#201](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/201))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/rust/ffi/libmpv.rs:24`, `docs/modules/mp3-player.md:239`
- Problem: The DLL is bound when the process loads through raw-dylib, so a missing libmpv-2.dll prevents the executable from starting. The module-level dependency declaration cannot prevent this.
- Impact: A missing DLL stops the whole shell, not only the music module.

```text
#[link(name = "libmpv-2", kind = "raw-dylib")]
```

### A184 MP3 player: model download hash is checked only when a header is sent; manifest never read

- Severity: high. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#202](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/202))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/rust/ai/model_fetch.rs:910`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/ai/model_fetch.rs:825`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/ai/model_fetch.rs:575`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/ai/model_fetch.rs:1080`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/ai/model_fetch.rs:202`
- Problem: SHA-256 is compared only when the server sends an X-Linked-ETag header, and no expected hash is pinned in code. Post-download validation checks only a file size threshold. The download manifest is written but never read back. The AI_DJ_MODEL_URL override is accepted with any URL scheme.
- Impact: A truncated or substituted model is accepted without a hash match, and it is then loaded in-process by ONNX Runtime.

```text
let value = response.header("x-linked-etag")?.trim().trim_matches('"');
if let Some(expected) = &expected_sha {
if !ai_dj::is_valid_onnx_model(&graph_path) {
source_url_with(std::env::var("AI_DJ_MODEL_URL").ok().as_deref(), file_name)
```

- Problem: A partial failure returns Ok with a warning. run_task then marks the playlist synced and sets the task to done.
- Impact: Missing tracks are not retried until the next sync interval, and the UI shows success.

```text
Err(detail) if recovered > 0 => Ok(YtDlpRun {
task.status = "done".to_string();
if let Err(error) = self.db.mark_playlist_synced(&task.playlist_id) {
```

### A186 MP3 player: config mutate releases its lock before writing to disk

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#204](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/204))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/rust/library/config.rs:124`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/library/config.rs:137`
- Problem: The lock is released before write_to_disk runs, although the doc comment states that the mutation and the write share one lock hold. Two concurrent updates can reach disk out of order.
- Impact: The file on disk can hold an older config than the one in memory.

```text
/// Targeted change under the lock: both the mutation and the write to disk happen under one
self.write_to_disk(&snapshot)?;
```

### A187 MP3 player: '#' in config values truncates library and tool paths

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#205](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/205)) Fixed in `d57697d`.
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/rust/library/config.rs:275`
- Problem: clean_value cuts every value at the first '#', including quoted paths. Text after a '#' in library_dir or yt_dlp_path is dropped.
- Impact: A library folder whose name contains '#' is read as a different path, so the library appears empty.

```text
let value = value.split('#').next().unwrap_or(value).trim();
```

### A188 MP3 player: crossfade setting is stored and clamped but never applied

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#206](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/206))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/rust/playback/player.rs:184`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/commands.rs:432`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/library/config.rs:190`
- Problem: The value is clamped and stored in player state, but no playback code reads crossfade_duration.
- Impact: The crossfade setting shown in the AI DJ settings has no audible effect.

```text
state.crossfade_duration = seconds.clamp(0.0, 10.0);
```

### A189 MP3 player: zoom-out below 0.25 zooms in because the clamps disagree

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#207](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/207))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/ui/widget.js:815`, `modules/mp3-player/ui/widget.js:1156`
- Problem: fitView can set zoom to 0.10, but the wheel handler's minimum is 0.25. Zooming out from a zoom below 0.25 sets it to 0.25, which increases zoom.
- Impact: Scrolling out on a fitted graph zooms in.

```text
zoom = Math.min(2.0, Math.max(0.10, Math.min((gw - 36) / bboxW, (gh - 36) / bboxH)));
const newZoom = Math.max(0.25, Math.min(5.0, zoom * zoomFactor));
```

### A190 MP3 player: embedding clear ignores store errors but clears memory anyway

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#208](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/208))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/rust/ai/ai_dj.rs:628`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/ai/ai_dj.rs:638`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/ai/ai_dj.rs:640`
- Problem: kv_list errors are dropped by the if-let and the batch result is discarded. The in-memory maps are cleared and the revision is bumped even when the store still holds the records.
- Impact: Memory and store disagree, and old embeddings can return after a restart.

```text
let _ = self.store.kv_batch(ops);
bump_analysis_revision();
```

### A191 MP3 player: failing AI DJ auto-advance retries every 500 ms and logs each time

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#209](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/209))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/rust/playback/player.rs:779`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/playback/player.rs:781`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/playback/player.rs:721`
- Problem: When AI DJ auto-advance returns an error, the function logs and returns without changing state. The end-of-file flag stays set, so the next probe repeats the same call.
- Impact: The log receives one line every 500 ms until the track changes or playback stops.

```text
format!("player: AI DJ auto-advance failed: {e}"),
return;
```

- Where: `modules/mp3-player/rust/sync/sync.rs:1114`
- Problem: The label is cut at byte 54 without a character-boundary check, so a multi-byte character spanning that byte panics.
- Impact: A pasted playlist URL with non-ASCII text makes the sync request fail with a task panic instead of a message.

```text
format!("{}...", &url[..54])
```

### A197 MP3 player: one-way shutdown latch blocks visualizer restart after shutdown

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#215](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/215))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/rust/commands.rs:108`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/commands.rs:892`
- Problem: IS_SHUTTING_DOWN is a process-wide one-way latch set by begin_shutdown. Once set, the visualizer cannot start and analysis loops stop, and nothing resets the latch.
- Impact: A module that is shut down and initialized again stays silent in the visualizer and stops analysis.
- Rule: `modules.lifecycle` (docs/reference/modules.md)

```text
if IS_SHUTTING_DOWN.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::SeqCst) {
return Ok(());
```

### A198 MP3 player: spectrum capture is dropped under the audio mutex, joining threads

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#216](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/216))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/rust/commands.rs:113`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/commands.rs:117`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/audio.rs:49`
- Problem: Disabling the visualizer drops AudioState while the audio mutex guard is held, and Drop joins both capture threads under that lock.
- Impact: The lock stays held while the threads finish, so any other visualizer call waits on it.

```text
(false, true) => {
*guard = None;
```

### A210 MP3 player: module depends on tauri directly and imports kernel UI internals

- Severity: medium. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#228](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/228))

- Problem: The crate depends on tauri directly and downcasts the host handle to tauri::AppHandle. The UI imports kernel UI modules (surface-manager.js, unidtk-core.js) by relative path.

### A215 MP3 player: sync URL is passed unvalidated as a yt-dlp argument

- Severity: medium. Type: security. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#233](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/233))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/rust/sync/sync.rs:87`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/sync/sync.rs:471`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/sync/sync.rs:602`
- Problem: The sync URL is trimmed and checked for emptiness only, then passed unchanged as a yt-dlp command-line argument. Values beginning with '-' are read by yt-dlp as options. The flaw comes from code reading and has not been run.
- Impact: yt-dlp runs with the user's browser cookie access and with options taken from the URL text.

```text
return Err("Paste a YouTube or YouTube Music playlist URL first".to_string());
command.arg(&task.source_url);
.arg(source_url)
```

### A216 MP3 player: playlist id is inserted unescaped into HTML attribute and selector

- Severity: medium. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#234](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/234))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/ui/widget.js:1713`, `modules/mp3-player/ui/widget.js:1929`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/library/db.rs:577`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/sync/sync.rs:90`
- Problem: The playlist id comes from the raw list= value of the pasted URL, with no charset check. It is placed unescaped into an innerHTML attribute and into a CSS attribute selector.
- Impact: A playlist id containing a quote character breaks the dashboard markup, and the rename selector throws.

```text
data-id="${p.id}"
const item = body.querySelector(`.music-playlist-item[data-id="${playlist.id}"]`);
```

### A217 MP3 player: status and timer loops keep polling for hidden or idle widgets

- Where: `modules/mp3-player/ui/widget.js:1635`, `modules/mp3-player/ui/widget.js:1637`, `modules/mp3-player/ui/audio-arcs.js:130`, `modules/mp3-player/ui/audio-arcs.js:250`
- Problem: Each card polls player_status every 2 s and gates only on document.hidden. The arcs loop keeps a 750 ms timer while any music widget is in the DOM and sends its own player_status every 2 s. The 250 ms ticker runs even when nothing plays.
- Impact: Collapsed or idle widgets keep waking the CPU and the IPC channel.

```text
card._musicSyncInterval = setInterval(async () => {
if (document.hidden) return;
invoke("player_status")
```

### A220 MP3 player: graph layout runs on the WebView main thread for up to 320 frames

- Severity: medium. Type: performance. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#238](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/238))

- Problem: The force layout runs as a requestAnimationFrame loop on the main thread for up to 320 frames. Each frame rebuilds a spatial grid and loops over all nodes and edges, with no cap on node count.
- Impact: A large analysed library makes the desktop UI unresponsive while the graph settles.

```text
const maxTicks = 320;
simAnimId = requestAnimationFrame(step);
```

### A221 MP3 player: AI DJ mutex is held during queue build, graph build and model load

- Severity: medium. Type: performance. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#239](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/239))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/rust/commands.rs:862`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/commands.rs:1126`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/commands.rs:1039`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/ai/ai_dj.rs:438`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/ai/ai_dj.rs:552`
- Problem: The player's AI DJ mutex is held during playlist queue ordering, similarity graph construction and the ONNX model load. Queue ordering and graph construction are both O(n²) over tracks.
- Impact: Status reads and analysis writes wait on the mutex for the whole computation.

```text
let ai_dj = player.ai_dj.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
return Ok(dj.build_similarity_graph(&tracks, 3));
ai_dj.ensure_session_ready()?;
```

- Where: `modules/mp3-player/rust/playback/player.rs:869`

### A227 MP3 player: Camelot compatibility slices an empty key and panics

- Where: `modules/mp3-player/rust/ai/ai_dj.rs:1088`


### A236 MP3 player: unwrap and expect calls can cascade a lock panic into later commands

- Severity: low. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#254](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/254))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/rust/commands.rs:747`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/playback/player.rs:270`
- Problem: The crate has about 140 unwrap and expect calls across seven files, including tests. Most lock accesses in player.rs use expect on a poisoned-lock message.
- Impact: A panic while a lock is held makes every later command that takes the same lock panic too.

```text
let prog = ANALYSIS_PROGRESS.read().unwrap();
```

### A237 MP3 player: status getter runs EOF auto-advance and clones the full state

- Severity: low. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#255](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/255))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/rust/playback/player.rs:714`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/playback/player.rs:715`
- Problem: status() can trigger auto-advance, which starts playback, and it clones the whole PlayerState, queue included, on every call.
- Impact: A status read can change what is playing, and each poll copies the full queue.

```text
self.maybe_auto_advance_eof();
```

### A241 MP3 player: theme colors, z-index fallbacks and inline shadow bypass tokens

- Severity: low. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#259](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/259))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/ui/widget.js:19`, `modules/mp3-player/ui/widget.js:186`, `modules/mp3-player/ui/widget.js:301`, `modules/mp3-player/ui/widget.js:2035`
- Problem: Hex and rgb fallbacks, literal z-index fallbacks (250 and 4000), and an inline box-shadow with literal rgba values bypass the token system.
- Impact: Theme changes do not reach these values, and dark or light variants can disagree.

```text
card.style.zIndex = "var(--z-surface-panel, 250)";
```

### A242 MP3 player: millisecond literals in timers and fade math bypass motion tokens

- Severity: low. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#260](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/260))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/ui/audio-arcs.js:214`, `modules/mp3-player/ui/audio-arcs.js:215`, `modules/mp3-player/ui/widget.js:1632`, `modules/mp3-player/ui/widget.js:1645`
- Problem: Millisecond literals (900, 620, 250, 2000, 1400) are written directly in timers and fade math instead of canonical duration tokens.
- Impact: Motion timing cannot be changed in one place.
- Rule: `motion.no-hardcoded-ms` (docs/reference/motion.md)

```text
if (lastBands && age < 900) {
const k = Math.max(0, 1 - (age - AUDIO_BANDS_FRESH_MS) / 620);
```

### A245 MP3 player: socket router names a write command in a match arm

- Severity: low. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#263](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/263))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/rust/lib.rs:40`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/lib.rs:41`
- Problem: A write command (model download) has a match arm in the socket router, while its comment says it is not registered on the socket. The kernel gate refuses the command before the arm is reached.
- Impact: The arm is dead code that contradicts the stated socket policy.
- Rule: `ipc.socket-read-only` (docs/reference/ipc.md)

```text
// Write command: it is reached only through the interface door, so we do not
// register it on the socket (this arm is called only from the paths mentioned above).
```

### A247 MP3 player: docs say EOF monitor ends with its last clone; line counts are stale

- Severity: low. Type: documentation. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#265](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/265))

- Problem: The chapter says the EOF monitor ends when other clones are dropped, but the kernel keeps a managed copy, so that condition does not occur. The AI DJ chapter gives line counts that differ from the current ai_dj.rs and model_fetch.rs.
- Impact: Readers get a wrong model of thread lifetime and file sizes.

```text
The loop ends when every other `PlayerManager` clone is gone, which is when the count of the shared `inner` drops to one.
```
