## VPN

### A249 VPN: connect silently switches to the first server when the selected server is missing

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#267](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/267))
- Where: `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1822`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1829`
- Problem: When the requested or remembered tag is absent, connect uses the first server in the list. Only a log line records the switch.
- Impact: The user gets a different server than the one requested, and the panel gives no message about it.

```text
// Target tag not in the current subscription (it may have been
// renamed/removed server-side). Fall back to the first server
// instead of failing the whole connect.
inner.servers.first().cloned()
```

### A250 VPN: watchdog clears the connected state after a core crash without stopping the TUN

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#268](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/268))
- Where: `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1745`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1771`
- Problem: The watchdog only resets the flags when the SOCKS port closes. It does not stop the helper or check that the TUN adapter is gone, and no traffic-blocking behaviour exists.
- Impact: The panel shows the VPN as off while the TUN may still capture all traffic; the adapter state after a crash is unverified.

```text
if inner.connected && !inner.connecting && !port_is_open(SOCKS_PORT) {
unidtk_api::log("vpn: watchdog detected proxy port closed while connected");
inner.connected = false;
```

### A251 VPN: quitting during connect can leave sing-box running because no stop is issued

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#269](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/269))
- Where: `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:2033`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1269`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1276`
- Problem: The shutdown check treats the VPN as idle when neither the SOCKS port nor the TUN exists. During bring-up the helper is already asked to start sing-box before either appears.
- Impact: A sing-box started through UniDTKSingbox can outlive the app and bring up a TUN with nothing to tear it down. Not reproduced at runtime.

```text
let nothing_up =
        !port_is_open(SOCKS_PORT) && !interface_is_connected(TUN_NAME).unwrap_or(false);
    if nothing_up {
        return true;
    }
```

### A252 VPN: saving a new subscription URL leaves the previous server list in use until restart

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#270](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/270))
- Where: `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:288`, `modules/vpn/ui/vpn-panel.js:122`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1695`
- Problem: The setter stores only the URL and does not clear the in-memory server list. The panel caches the list for the session, and refresh_servers has no UI caller.
- Impact: Server choices come from the old subscription until the app restarts.

```text
pub fn set_subscription_url(url: String) -> Result<VpnSettingsStatus, String> {
    let mut settings = load_settings();
    settings.subscription_url = url.trim().to_string();
```

### A253 VPN: subscription save toast says the server list was refreshed, which is not true

- Severity: high. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#271](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/271))
- Where: `core/ui/menu.js:1100`, `core/ui/menu.js:1101`
- Problem: The success toast claims the server list was updated. The call only saves the URL, and no list refresh follows.
- Impact: The user is told the new servers are active when the old list is still in use.

```text
await invoke("vpn_set_subscription_url", { url: pane.querySelector("#vpnSubUrl").value.trim() });
showToast("Подписка сохранена, список серверов обновлён");
```

### A254 VPN: panel never shows the stop reason or proxy error text, only a generic label

- Severity: high. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#272](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/272))
- Where: `modules/vpn/ui/vpn-panel.js:311`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:351`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1752`
- Problem: The panel does not read the error field of the status. proxyError is shown only as the word for error. The stop reason is set in last_error and never rendered.
- Impact: After an unexpected stop or a failed probe, the user sees a generic off or error label without the reason.

```text
if (label) {
      label.textContent = status.connecting
        ? "VPN: подключение…"
```

### A255 VPN: subscription fetch runs while the runtime mutex is held, blocking status polling

- Severity: high. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#273](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/273))
- Where: `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1527`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1688`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1801`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:532`
- Problem: refresh_servers_locked performs a blocking HTTP fetch with a 15 s timeout while holding the runtime mutex. status() uses the same mutex.
- Impact: Status polling, and so the panel label and toggle, can freeze for up to 15 s during a fetch.

```text
let mut inner = runtime.inner.lock().unwrap();
if inner.servers.is_empty() {
    refresh_servers_locked(&mut inner)?;
```

### A256 VPN: TLS outbounds are generated with insecure: true, so server certificates are not verified

- Severity: high. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#274](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/274))
- Where: `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:611`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:495`
- Problem: The TLS branch sets insecure to true for every server with security tls. Trojan links without a security parameter default to tls.
- Impact: Any on-path party can impersonate the proxy and read or alter traffic captured by the TUN, while the panel shows connected.

```text
} else if server.security == "tls" {
        outbound["tls"] = json!({
            "enabled": true,
            "server_name": server.sni,
            "insecure": true,
```

### A257 VPN: subscription URL token can reach the application log through an unredacted request error

- Severity: high. Type: security. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#275](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/275))
- Where: `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:534`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1714`, `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:1007`
- Problem: The subscription fetch error is formatted into the log and returned to the UI without redaction. The micrographics module redacts request URLs from such errors, the VPN module does not.
- Impact: The subscription token, which is part of the URL, is written to the application log that udtk logs reads.

```text
.map_err(|e| format!("subscription request failed: {e}"))?;
unidtk_api::log(format!("vpn: fetch_servers failed: {e}"));
```

### A258 VPN: connect is not a no-op when already connected and restarts the running core

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#276](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/276))
- Where: `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1802`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1229`, `modules/vpn/ui/vpn-panel.js:257`
- Problem: connect guards only the connecting flag. A connect while connected reaches bring_up, which calls perform_stop on the live core.
- Impact: Repeating connect, for example by picking the current server, drops and restarts the tunnel.
- Rule: `modules.idempotent` (docs/reference/modules.md)

```text
if inner.connecting {
            unidtk_api::log("vpn: connect ignored: already connecting");
    ...
    let stop_nonce = perform_stop();
```

### A259 VPN: DNS for all names, including .ru, goes to 8.8.8.8 through the proxy

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#277](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/277))
- Where: `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:722`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:709`, `docs/modules/vpn.md:430`
- Problem: The config defines one DNS server and no DNS rules, so every query goes through the proxy to Google DNS. The docs warn against this for Russian local traffic.
- Impact: Russian names resolve to foreign CDN nodes, and the .ru direct route does not apply to name resolution.

```text
"servers": [{ "type": "tcp", "tag": "dns-proxy", "server": "8.8.8.8", "detour": "proxy" }]
```

### A260 VPN: helper stops sing-box with TerminateProcess, skipping graceful cleanup

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#278](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/278))
- Where: `modules/vpn/helper/main.rs:200`
- Problem: The stop is a hard kill, so sing-box's cleanup of routes, DNS and the TUN adapter does not run. Whether those are left behind is unverified.
- Impact: If confirmed, a stop can leave routing or DNS pointing into a dead TUN.

```text
let _ = TerminateProcess(handle, 0);
        // Bounded wait: the caller (elevated task) must not linger, and the
        // app side re-verifies through the port/TUN checks anyway.
        let _ = WaitForSingleObject(handle, 2000);
```

- Problem: Only vless and trojan lines are parsed, and every other scheme returns None without a message.
- Impact: A mixed subscription connects with fewer servers than it lists, and the user is not told.

```text
                parse_vless_line(l)
```

### A262 VPN: connected state is committed even when the transport probe failed

- Severity: medium. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#280](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/280))
- Where: `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1871`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1875`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1328`
- Problem: The transport probe is advisory, and a failed HTTP probe still sets connected to true. The probe is not repeated afterward.
- Impact: The panel can show the VPN as on with no evidence that traffic passes through the tunnel.

```text
inner.connected = true;
inner.proxy_error = outcome.probe_error;
```

### A263 VPN: missing scheduled task surfaces only as raw schtasks stderr in a short toast

- Severity: medium. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#281](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/281))
- Where: `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:811`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1070`
- Problem: The pre-flight check only tests that the .exe files exist, not that the tasks are registered or that elevation works. The failure text is the raw schtasks stderr.
- Impact: A missing task and an elevation failure look the same and are not actionable for the user; the message shows for about 4 seconds.

```text
if !output.status.success() {
        return Err(format!("failed to start {task_name}: {stderr}"));
    }
```

### A264 VPN: the check-all button also connects to the fastest server

- Severity: medium. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#282](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/282))
- Where: `modules/vpn/ui/vpn-panel.js:191`
- Problem: The check-all action, which measures latency, calls connectTo with the fastest result. A current connection to another server is replaced.
- Impact: A test click drops the current tunnel and starts a different one.

```text
if (working.length) {
        const best = working[0];
        await connectTo(best.tag);
```

### A265 VPN: failed subscription refresh silently serves an old or cached server list

- Severity: medium. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#283](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/283))
- Where: `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1718`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1722`
- Problem: When the fetch fails, the module uses the in-memory or cached list and records only a log line. The UI shows nothing.
- Impact: The user sees a stale server list as current and can connect to servers that no longer exist.

```text
if !inner.servers.is_empty() {
                unidtk_api::log("vpn: keeping in-memory servers after failed refresh");
            }
            let cached = load_servers_cache();
```

### A266 VPN: module shutdown ignores the 1500 ms budget and fires the stop without waiting

- Severity: medium. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#284](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/284))
- Where: `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:2213`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:2011`, `docs/modules/vpn.md:346`
- Problem: The budget argument is unused. The call routes to the zero-duration path that returns before the helper finishes.
- Impact: The exit path never confirms that sing-box was stopped.
- Rule: `modules.lifecycle` (docs/reference/modules.md)

```text
fn shutdown(&self, _budget: std::time::Duration) -> Result<(), String> {
        shutdown(&self.runtime);
        Ok(())
    }
```

### A267 VPN: country routing rule covers only the .ru suffix, not the Russian-zone punycode suffix

- Severity: medium. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#285](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/285))
- Where: `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:664`, `docs/modules/vpn.md:414`
- Problem: Only the .ru suffix routes direct, and the country rule is compiled in. The Russian-zone suffix .рф (punycode form) is not included, though the Happ appendix lists it.
- Impact: Russian-zone names outside .ru go through the proxy, and the rule cannot be changed without a rebuild.

```text
// Keep Russian domains on the normal network interface; all
        // other TUN traffic uses `proxy`.
        json!({ "outbound": "direct", "domain_suffix": [".ru"] }),
```

### A268 VPN: home-server defaults are a personal LAN subnet and disagree with the settings page

- Severity: medium. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#286](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/286))
- Where: `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:61`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:689`, `core/ui/menu.js:1084`
- Problem: The backend default subnet is 192.168.31.0/24, a personal LAN address. The settings page uses 10.10.0.0/16 as its default.
- Impact: The two defaults disagree, and a user on another LAN gets an unrelated direct route when the submodule is enabled.

```text
fn default_home_subnet() -> String {
    "192.168.31.0/24".to_string()
}
```

### A269 VPN: routing, DNS, subscription count and kill switch have no user setting

- Severity: medium. Type: customization. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#287](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/287))
- Where: `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:109`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:664`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:722`
- Problem: Only one subscription URL is stored. Routing is one fixed .ru rule, DNS is fixed, and no kill switch or per-app or per-domain rules exist.
- Impact: Users cannot tune routing, DNS, or the set of subscriptions from the interface.

```text
subscription_url: String,
```

### A270 VPN: elevated helper runs a sing-box config from a user-writable data directory

- Severity: medium. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#288](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/288))
- Where: `modules/vpn/helper/main.rs:79`, `modules/vpn/helper/main.rs:96`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:66`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:803`
- Problem: The helper runs sing-box with a config file in %LOCALAPPDATA% that any process of the same user can write. Starting the task needs no elevation. The helper reads the stop nonce and pid from the same directory.
- Impact: A same-user process can run an elevated sing-box with a substituted config; the impact depends on the UAC setting.

```text
let config = dir.join("singbox_config.json");
match Command::new(&exe)
    .args(["run", "-c"])
    .arg(&config)
```

### A271 VPN: sing-box info-level log is copied into the application log on failure

- Severity: medium. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#289](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/289))
- Where: `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:708`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1289`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1323`
- Problem: The core log runs at info level, which logs each routed connection. On failure its last 40 lines are copied into the application log.
- Impact: Destinations of the user's traffic persist in the application log.

```text
"log": { "level": "info", "output": singbox_log, "timestamp": true },
log_tail_file("singbox", &singbox_log_path(), 40);
```

### A272 VPN: generated sing-box config with server credentials is written as plain JSON

- Severity: medium. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#290](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/290))
- Where: `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1247`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:84`
- Problem: The config holds server identifiers and key material in plain text, including the home-server id, REALITY public key and short id. It is written to the app data directory.
- Impact: Server credentials persist unencrypted in the user's data directory.

```text
std::fs::write(
        singbox_config_path(),
        serde_json::to_string_pretty(&singbox_cfg).unwrap_or_default(),
```

### A273 VPN: interface check spawns cmd.exe and netsh on every poll during stop and connect waits

- Severity: medium. Type: performance. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#291](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/291))
- Where: `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1185`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:821`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1277`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1195`
- Problem: Each interface check starts cmd.exe and netsh. The stop wait checks every 150 ms for up to 10 s.
- Impact: Up to about 66 process spawns per stop wait, and process churn during connect.

```text
let tun_up = interface_is_connected(TUN_NAME).unwrap_or(false);
    let output = Command::new("cmd.exe")
```

### A274 VPN: runtime mutex unwrap and connecting flag can leave the module unusable after a panic

- Severity: low. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#292](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/292))
- Where: `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1528`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1744`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1833`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1841`
- Problem: About 22 lock sites use unwrap, and connecting is cleared only after bring_up returns, with no panic guard.
- Impact: After a panic, every later status or connect call panics, or connect reports already in progress until restart.

```text
let mut inner = runtime.inner.lock().unwrap();
inner.connecting = true;
let result = bring_up(&server);
```

### A275 VPN: ping treats any HTTP error status from a probe endpoint as a successful result

- Severity: low. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#293](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/293))
- Where: `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1485`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1493`
- Problem: Any HTTP error status counts as success with a latency value, including 5xx or a blocked endpoint's 403.
- Impact: A server whose path is blocked to the probe endpoints can show a latency and be auto-selected.
- Rule: `modules.idempotent` (docs/reference/modules.md)

```text
// Even an error status (4xx/5xx) proves the tunnel works
                return Ok(elapsed.min(u32::MAX as u128) as u32);
```

### A277 VPN: timeouts, probe endpoints, ports and TUN addressing are compiled in

- Problem: User-Agent strings, probe endpoints, a SOCKS port, the TUN name, address and MTU, and many timeouts and retry counts are literals with no setting.

### A280 VPN: stop task description says it matches the command line, but the helper matches image path

- Severity: low. Type: documentation. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#298](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/298))
- Where: `scheduler-tasks/UniDTKVpnStopTask.xml:8`, `modules/vpn/helper/main.rs:181`
- Problem: The XML description says the stop matches the UniDTK config command line. The helper compares the process image path.

```text
<Description>Elevated helper: stops the wallpaper VPN sing-box process by matching its UniDTK config command line.</Description>
```

## Micrographics

### A281 Micrographics: refresh and retry timers are fixed constants, and the DOM repaints every second

- Severity: medium. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#299](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/299))
- Where: `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:46`, `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:59`, `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:1817`, `modules/micrographics/ui/widget.js:369`, `modules/micrographics/ui/widget.js:382`
- Problem: Weather, IP and name cadences, the 20 s geolocation wait and retry delays are literals. The widget repaints every second and pauses only on document hidden.
- Impact: Connected widgets repaint every second, and none of the cadences can be configured.

```text
const REFRESH: Duration = Duration::from_secs(15 * 60);
const LOCATION_FOR: u64 = 30 * 60;
```

### A282 Micrographics: stale thresholds are duplicated in JS and Rust and can disagree

- Severity: medium. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#300](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/300))
- Where: `modules/micrographics/ui/composition.js:41`, `modules/micrographics/ui/composition.js:152`, `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:55`
- Problem: The JS side hard-codes 1200 s and 1800 s staleness, and the Rust side has its own constants. The same 1000 m accuracy threshold appears in both.
- Impact: Changing a Rust constant does not change the UI's stale decision, so the two can disagree.

```text
return { stale: Boolean(weather?.stale) || minutes === null || now.getTime() / 1000 - weather.observedAt > 1200, age };
const FRESH_FOR: u64 = 20 * 60;
```

### A283 Micrographics: provider endpoints, User-Agent and Russian language parameters are fixed

- Severity: medium. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#301](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/301))
- Where: `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:86`, `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:508`, `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:532`, `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:539`, `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:766`, `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:770`, `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:1031`
- Problem: Five providers are hard-coded with their URLs, the User-Agent is a literal, and place-name language is fixed to Russian.
- Impact: The provider cannot be changed, and place names are always returned in Russian.

```text
const UA: &str = "unidtk-micrographics/1.0";
        .query("localityLanguage", "ru")
        .query("accept-language", "ru")
```

### A284 Micrographics: units, location override, refresh period and provider are not settings

- Severity: medium. Type: customization. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#302](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/302))
- Where: `modules/micrographics/ui/composition.js:83`, `modules/micrographics/ui/composition.js:95`, `modules/micrographics/ui/metadata.json:118`
- Problem: Temperature is always °C and wind always km/h. The only lookOptions field is mgLayout, with no location, unit or refresh setting.
- Impact: Users cannot choose °F or m/s or set a city, and cannot change how often data updates.

```text
add("weather:temperature", "temperature", f.stale ? "off" : "val", `${signed(weather.tempC)}°C`, source);
"field": "mgLayout",
```

### A285 Micrographics: public IP lookup runs before the location-consent check on every cycle

- Severity: medium. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#303](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/303))
- Where: `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:1522`, `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:1531`, `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:766`, `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:770`
- Problem: The IP lookup (ipapi.co with ipwho.is fallback) runs before the location-permission check. The result is stored in the module cache.
- Impact: The user's public IP and city are sent to two third-party services with no opt-out, even when location access was denied.

```text
if Instant::now() >= ip_clock.due {
            let result = lookup_ip_location();
```

### A286 Micrographics: device coordinates are sent to three providers and stored with lat/lon

- Severity: medium. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#304](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/304))
- Where: `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:505`, `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:537`, `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:1027`, `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:1646`, `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:1448`
- Problem: Exact coordinates are sent in query strings to BigDataCloud, Nominatim and Open-Meteo. The place fix is stored with latitude and longitude and restored without an age check.
- Impact: Precise location goes to three providers with no per-provider choice, and a coordinate pair persists in the module store.

```text
let response = http_agent().get("https://api.bigdatacloud.net/data/reverse-geocode-client")
        .query("latitude", &fix.latitude.to_string())
        .query("longitude", &fix.longitude.to_string())
```

### A287 Micrographics: one 429 without Retry-After silences the IP indicator for 24 hours

- Severity: low. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#305](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/305))
- Where: `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:742`, `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:1496`
- Problem: Without a Retry-After header, a 429 sets a 24 h cooldown for the IP provider. It is cleared only by restart or a network change.
- Impact: The IP indicator disappears for a day after a single rate-limit answer.

```text
let retry = response.header("Retry-After").and_then(|value| value.parse::<u64>().ok())
                    .unwrap_or(if *status == 429 { 86400 } else { LOCATION_FOR }).clamp(60, 86400);
```

### A288 Micrographics: reads selected_tag from VPN events, which never carry it

- Severity: low. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#306](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/306))
- Where: `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:1967`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:24`
- Problem: The VPN module publishes only the connected flag, so selected_tag is always absent. The route comparison uses the flag alone.
- Impact: The code reads as if it tracks the server, which it does not; the comparison is misleading.

```text
let route = (connected,
                payload.get("selected_tag").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).map(str::to_string));
```

### A289 Micrographics: each init adds event subscriptions that cannot be removed

- Severity: low. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#307](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/307))
- Where: `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:1956`, `core/api/src/lib.rs:226`
- Problem: subscribe has no unsubscribe, and init subscribes again on every switch. Handlers hold Weak references.
- Impact: Handlers accumulate across suspend and resume cycles without causing a crash.
- Rule: `modules.lifecycle` (docs/reference/modules.md)

```text
for event in ["network-changed", "system-resumed"] {
            let weak = Arc::downgrade(&state);
            unidtk_api::subscribe(event, move |_| {
```

### A290 Micrographics: each uncached DNS lookup starts a thread that outlives its timeout

- Severity: low. Type: architecture. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#308](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/308))
- Where: `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:133`, `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:138`
- Problem: Each uncached lookup spawns a new thread. When the 4 s timeout fires, the thread keeps running until the system resolver returns.
- Impact: Thread count grows with refresh attempts while the resolver is blocked; the duration is unverified.

```text
let _ = thread::Builder::new().name("weather-dns".into()).spawn(move || {
...
let addrs: Vec<SocketAddr> = receive.recv_timeout(HTTP_TIMEOUT)
```

### A291 Micrographics: a detached reaper lets the weather worker outlive shutdown

- Severity: low. Type: architecture. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#309](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/309))
- Where: `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:1348`, `modules/micrographics/rust/micrographics.rs:1335`
- Problem: stop() returns without waiting, and a detached thread joins the worker later. The worker can still be in an HTTP call or device lookup.
- Impact: Network work can continue after the module reports shutdown; the exact duration is unverified.
- Rule: `modules.lifecycle` (docs/reference/modules.md)

```text
.name("weather-reaper".to_string())
```

### A292 Micrographics: labels, months and statuses are Russian and time is forced to 24 h

- Severity: low. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#310](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/310))
- Where: `modules/micrographics/ui/composition.js:4`, `modules/micrographics/ui/composition.js:50`, `modules/micrographics/ui/composition.js:134`
- Problem: Labels, months, days and status strings are literals in Russian. The clock format is pinned to h23 with ru-RU.
- Impact: Users in other languages or with a 12 h preference cannot change the presentation.

```text
const MONTHS = ["ЯНВ", "ФЕВ", "МАР", "АПР", "МАЙ", "ИЮН", "ИЮЛ", "АВГ", "СЕН", "ОКТ", "НОЯ", "ДЕК"];
```

## Glyph bar

### A293 Glyph bar: volume is set optimistically and not restored when the call fails

- Severity: medium. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#311](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/311))
- Where: `modules/glyph-bar/ui/widget.js:200`, `modules/glyph-bar/ui/widget.js:202`
- Problem: LEDs update before the call. On failure the catch shows a toast and leaves the new state in place. The same pattern applies to mute, drag and wheel.
- Impact: The bar shows a level that the device did not accept until the next volume change.

```text
systemVolumeState = { level: target, muted: false };
    applyGlyphBarVisualAll();
    const v = await invoke("set_system_volume", { level: target });
```

### A294 Glyph bar: initial volume read is never retried and a missing device shows no state

- Severity: medium. Type: ux. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#312](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/312))
- Where: `modules/glyph-bar/ui/widget.js:143`, `modules/glyph-bar/ui/widget.js:177`, `modules/glyph-bar/rust/glyph_bar.rs:88`
- Problem: The first read runs once and is not retried if it fails. The poll loop logs one error per second with no backoff, and the widget has no no-device state.
- Impact: The bar can stay at zero until the volume changes, with no indication that a device is missing.

```text
if (!systemVolumeReady) refreshSystemVolume();
console.warn("getSystemVolume failed", err);
```

### A295 Glyph bar: shutdown joins the poll thread without a timeout

- Severity: medium. Type: architecture. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#313](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/313))
- Where: `modules/glyph-bar/rust/glyph_bar.rs:40`, `modules/glyph-bar/rust/glyph_bar.rs:92`, `core/kernel/main.rs:137`
- Problem: stop() joins the poll thread with no timeout, and that thread can sleep one second or block in a COM call. The thread the kernel calls this on is unverified.
- Impact: Shutdown can stall for the join time if the caller is the UI thread.

```text
self.stop.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
if let Ok(mut threads) = self.threads.lock() {
    for thread in threads.drain(..) {
        let _ = thread.join();
```

### A296 Glyph bar: expect calls can panic the poll thread or the module lock

- Severity: low. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#314](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/314))
- Where: `modules/glyph-bar/rust/glyph_bar.rs:98`, `modules/glyph-bar/rust/glyph_bar.rs:328`, `modules/glyph-bar/rust/glyph_bar.rs:333`
- Problem: expect is used in the poll loop and in init and shutdown on the module lock.
- Impact: A panic in the poll thread stops volume updates for the rest of the session with no restart.

```text
let reading = endpoint.as_ref().map(read_endpoint).expect("endpoint opened above");
```

### A297 Glyph bar: the level mapping has no 10% step, so 10% cannot be set by clicking

- Severity: low. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#315](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/315))
- Where: `modules/glyph-bar/ui/widget.js:15`, `modules/glyph-bar/ui/widget.js:198`
- Problem: Index 1 maps to 20 percent, so the 10 percent level has no segment.
- Impact: The bar cannot select 10 percent by clicking.

```text
if (index <= 0) return 0;
  return Math.min(100, (index + 1) * 10);
```

### A298 Glyph bar: segment count, gap, size limits and device selection are compiled in

- Severity: low. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#316](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/316))
- Where: `modules/glyph-bar/ui/widget.js:4`, `modules/glyph-bar/rust/glyph_bar.rs:221`, `modules/glyph-bar/ui/widget.js:85`
- Problem: Segment count, gap and side limits are constants. The audio device is always the default endpoint, and there is no hide timeout. Segment aria-labels are Russian.
- Impact: Users cannot change the segment count, the device, or hiding behaviour.

```text
const GB_SEGMENTS = 10;
const GB_GAP = 3;
.GetDefaultAudioEndpoint(eRender, eConsole)
```

### A299 Glyph bar: volume endpoint is polled every 120 ms for the whole session

- Severity: low. Type: performance. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#317](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/317))
- Where: `modules/glyph-bar/rust/glyph_bar.rs:18`, `modules/glyph-bar/rust/glyph_bar.rs:113`, `modules/glyph-bar/rust/glyph_bar.rs:20`
- Problem: The poll runs about eight times per second for the module's lifetime, including when the widget is in background or hidden. Device recheck runs every 16 polls.
- Impact: Constant background wake-ups for a control that is usually idle, with no setting for the rate.

```text
const POLL: Duration = Duration::from_millis(120);
        thread::sleep(POLL);
```

## Clock

- Severity: medium. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#318](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/318))
- Where: `modules/clock/ui/widget.js:98`
- Problem: The analogue-dot style renders hard-coded numbers for calories and steps, plus a fixed date. No data source backs them.
- Impact: Invented activity figures appear on the clock as if they were real.

```text
СБ 23<br>
156 ккал<br>
6 240 шагов
```

### A301 Clock: dates use ru-RU, the dashboard month uses the system locale, and time is 24 h only

- Severity: medium. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#319](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/319))
- Where: `modules/clock/ui/widget.js:182`, `modules/clock/ui/widget.js:253`, `modules/clock/ui/widget.js:263`, `modules/clock/ui/gooey.js:52`
- Problem: The date locale is hard-coded to ru-RU, while the dashboard month name uses the system default. The calendar starts on Sunday with ambiguous one-letter day names, and time is always 24 h.
- Impact: Users cannot pick a locale, 12 h time, Monday-first weeks or another time zone.

```text
const dateStr = now.toLocaleDateString("ru-RU", {
```

### A302 Clock: dashboard online status is read once at render and never updates

- Severity: low. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#320](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/320))
- Where: `modules/clock/ui/widget.js:274`, `modules/clock/ui/widget.js:289`
- Problem: navigator.onLine is captured once when the dashboard renders, with no online or offline listener.
- Impact: The status text is stale until the dashboard is rendered again.

```text
const isOnline = navigator.onLine;
<div class="net-status">${isOnline ? 'В сети' : 'Нет сети'}</div>
```

### A303 Clock: global one-second interval pauses only on document hidden, not on the card

- Severity: low. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#321](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/321))
- Where: `modules/clock/ui/widget.js:169`, `modules/clock/ui/widget.js:170`
- Problem: The tick is stored on window and is paused only by document.hidden. Each tick queries the whole document for every clock style.
- Impact: The clock keeps querying while it is off the desk, and the global state is shared.

```text
window.clockInterval = setInterval(() => {
    if (document.hidden) return;
    updateClockTime();
```

### A304 Clock: inline z-index values in the analogue-dot face bypass the token system

- Severity: low. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#322](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/322))
- Where: `modules/clock/ui/widget.js:95`, `modules/clock/ui/widget.js:96`, `modules/clock/ui/widget.js:97`
- Problem: Three inline elements set z-index 10, 8 and 7 directly in the JS template.
- Impact: The layer order is not controlled by the theme tokens.
- Rule: `theme.tokens-only` (docs/reference/desk.md)

```text
<div class="clock-center" style="width: 8px; height: 8px; background: rgba(var(--fg-rgb), 0.2); z-index: 10;"></div>
```

### A305 Clock: third-party brand name is hard-coded into the minimal watch face

- Severity: low. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#323](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/323))
- Where: `modules/clock/ui/widget.js:126`
- Problem: The text CMF BY NOTHING is a literal in the template and cannot be changed or removed.
- Impact: Brand text ships in the UI with no setting.

```text
<div style="position: absolute; bottom: 15%; font-family: var(--font-accent); font-size: 0.6rem; color: var(--theme-accent);">CMF BY NOTHING</div>
```

## Kernel and contract

### A306 Kernel: tray Exit and ExitRequested skip waiting for the elevated VPN helper

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#324](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/324))
- Where: `core/kernel/main.rs:2451`, `core/kernel/main.rs:2568`, `core/kernel/main.rs:151`, `core/kernel/shutdown.rs:6`
- Problem: Tray Exit and RunEvent::ExitRequested call app_shutdown with wait_for_vpn set to false, so the helper is not waited on. quit_app and the session-end path do wait.
- Impact: sing-box can survive exit with its TUN up, which the shutdown.rs comment says black-holes the network on the next boot.

```text
"quit" => {
app.exit(0);
app_shutdown(app, false);
with the session while `sing-box` keeps its TUN up — and a surviving TUN
```

### A307 Kernel: undecodable folder records vanish from the UI with no message

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#325](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/325))
- Where: `core/kernel/unidtk_db.rs:213`, `core/kernel/unidtk_db.rs:151`, `core/kernel/unidtk_db.rs:123`
- Problem: Folder records that fail to decode are dropped from the list. The sort fallback message goes to eprintln, which is discarded in the windows-subsystem build. Tabs, sort and disabled modules use unversioned bincode.
- Impact: Folders disappear from the UI without a message, and a schema change silently resets the stored values.

```text
if let Ok(record) = serde_json::from_slice::<FolderRecord>(val.value()) {
eprintln!("WARN: Failed to deserialize sort, falling back to default: {e}");
```

### A308 Kernel: a panic in the tao event loop on every Windows session end, shutdown never completes

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: reproduced on a live build. ([#326](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/326))
- Where: `core/kernel/shutdown.rs:52`, `core/kernel/shutdown.rs:60`, `core/kernel/main.rs:129`, `core/kernel/panic_hook.rs:14`
- Problem: In the journal, 3 of 3 observed session ends (WM_QUERYENDSESSION, WM_ENDSESSION, `shutdown: begin`) are followed by this panic on the main thread; crash.log holds 50 such records. After it the journal never shows `shutdown: done`. The root cause is not proven: the release build is stripped, so the backtrace has only <unknown> frames.
- Impact: Every logoff or shutdown ends in a crash instead of a clean stop, and the VPN stop and log flush may not finish in that path.

```text
thread 'main' panicked at tao-0.35.3\src\platform_impl\windows\event_loop\runner.rs:371:25
cannot move state from Destroyed
```

- Impact: The native Explorer shortcut stops working system-wide, and no setting restores it.

```text
let hotkey = get_registry().try_read().ok().and_then(|keys| keys.iter().find(|key|
swallow: true,
keybd_event(0xE8, 0, Default::default(), 0);
if key.swallow {
```

### A310 Kernel: failed wallpaper attach leaves widgets hidden and only logs the failure

- Severity: high. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#328](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/328))
- Where: `core/kernel/main.rs:1578`, `core/kernel/main.rs:1573`, `core/kernel/main.rs:1627`, `core/kernel/main.rs:1645`
- Problem: On attach failure the window stays hidden. The retry loop stops after 40 attempts, about 27 s, and records only a log line.
- Impact: The widgets never appear, no message is shown, and nothing retries after the loop ends.

```text
let _ = window.hide();
log_line(format!("show_and_attach: attach failed; window stays hidden: {error}"));
for attempt in 0..40 {
log_line("request_wallpaper_visible: desktop host unavailable after 40 attempts");
```

### A311 Kernel: startup build failure exits with no user-visible message

- Severity: high. Type: ux. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#329](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/329))
- Where: `core/kernel/main.rs:2562`, `core/kernel/main.rs:2561`, `core/kernel/panic_hook.rs:27`, `core/kernel/main.rs:1`
- Problem: The Tauri build uses expect, and the resulting panic is recorded only in crash.log. The release binary uses the windows subsystem, so there is no console output.
- Impact: A failed start, for example from a missing WebView2 runtime (not verified in this audit), shows no window, tray icon or dialog.

```text
.build(tauri::generate_context!())
.expect("tauri build")
write_crash_log(&report);
#![windows_subsystem = "windows"]
```

### A312 Kernel: main.rs hardcodes module ids and names in shutdown, setup and layout

- Severity: high. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#330](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/330))
- Where: `core/kernel/main.rs:10`, `core/kernel/main.rs:133`, `core/kernel/main.rs:185`, `core/kernel/main.rs:596`, `core/kernel/main.rs:2426`, `core/kernel/main.rs:522`
- Problem: The kernel hardcodes module ids and crate names in shutdown, startup, the enable switch and the default layout. Roughly 170 Tauri wrappers forward module commands.
- Impact: Adding or removing a module requires editing the kernel in several places.
- Rule: `kernel.core-invisible` (docs/reference/kernel.md)

```text
use unidtk_mod_telegram as telegram;
let ctx = module_context(&handle, "mp3-player", true);
"telegram" => switch_module::<telegram::TelegramModule>(app, id, enabled)?,
```

### A313 Kernel: weather flow, mp3 table layouts and explorer data live in kernel code

- Severity: high. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#331](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/331))
- Where: `core/kernel/events.rs:34`, `core/kernel/weather_location.rs:6`, `core/kernel/library_migration.rs:22`, `core/kernel/unidtk_db.rs:23`, `core/kernel/ipc.rs:597`, `core/kernel/registry.rs:13`, `core/kernel/hotkeys.rs:55`
- Problem: The kernel contains a weather permission flow, mp3-player table layouts, explorer tab and folder storage, module event names and a module list. hotkeys.rs names the Shell Explorer hotkey.
- Impact: Removing or changing these features requires kernel changes instead of changes to the module.
- Rule: `kernel.core-invisible` (docs/reference/kernel.md)

```text
"vpn",
const LABEL: &str = "weather-location-permission";
const MODULE_ID: &str = "mp3-player";
const EXPLORER_TABS: TableDefinition<&str, &[u8]> = TableDefinition::new("explorer_tabs");
```

### A314 Kernel: DevTools port is always on without auth and the page token is readable

- Severity: high. Type: security. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#332](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/332))
- Where: `core/kernel/main.rs:2121`, `core/kernel/main.rs:1780`, `core/kernel/ipc.rs:373`, `core/kernel/main.rs:1821`, `core/kernel/ws.rs:11`
- Problem: The WebView2 DevTools port is enabled on every start and the kernel does not authenticate clients of it. The port is published in the unauthenticated socket reply to ping, and session_info returns the WS door token to the page.
- Impact: Any local process can drive the WebView and invoke every registered Tauri command, including file deletion, shortcut launch and message sending.
- Rule: `ipc.cdp-always-on` (docs/reference/ipc.md)

```text
// our CLI sends no Origin header, and a client-chosen Origin is not a control.
let cdp_port = pick_cdp_port();
if std::net::TcpListener::bind(("127.0.0.1", PREFERRED)).is_ok() {
"cdpPort": crate::cdp_port(),
"token": ws::token(),
```

### A315 Kernel: socket-served vpn_list_servers fetches over HTTP and writes cache under a lock

- Severity: high. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#333](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/333))
- Where: `core/kernel/command_inventory.rs:115`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:1690`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:532`
- Problem: The command is declared read-only and is served on the socket. When the cache is empty it fetches the subscription URL over HTTP with a 15 s timeout, saves the cache, and holds the runtime mutex during the fetch.
- Impact: Unauthenticated local calls trigger outbound requests that use the subscription, and can block other callers that need the same lock.
- Rule: `ipc.socket-read-only` (docs/reference/ipc.md)

```text
CommandSpec::read_only("vpn_list_servers", "VPN servers", "");
let mut inner = runtime.inner.lock().unwrap();
if inner.servers.is_empty() {
refresh_servers_locked(&mut inner)?;
```

### A316 Socket door: `vpn_settings` returns the VPN subscription URL to any local process

- Severity: high. Type: security. Status: reproduced on a live build. ([#334](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/334))
- Where: `core/kernel/command_inventory.rs:98`, `core/kernel/ipc.rs:627`, `modules/vpn/rust/vpn.rs:356`
- Problem: `vpn_settings` is declared read-only and is served on the unauthenticated loopback socket. A live `udtk invoke vpn_settings` returned `subscription_url` in full (58 characters, a URL that carries an access token).
- Impact: Any local process can read the user's VPN subscription credential without any permission.
- Rule: `ipc.socket-read-only` (docs/reference/ipc.md)

```text
pub subscription_url: String,
```

### A317 Kernel: log level filter is case-sensitive, so `udtk logs --level ERROR` always returns nothing

- Severity: high. Type: tooling. Status: reproduced on a live build. ([#335](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/335)) Fixed in `966febd`.
- Where: `core/kernel/log_store.rs:133`, `opt/cli/main.rs:1514`, `opt/cli/main.rs:3328`, `docs/udtk/ch08-invoke-and-logs.md:37`
- Problem: Stored levels are upper case ("ERROR"). The filter lower-cases the stored level but not the requested one, so the upper-case form that the docs and AGENTS.md mandate never matches. On the live build `--level ERROR` returned [] while `--level error` returned 3 records.
- Impact: The ERROR check in `udtk build` (final report), the frontend error audit in `udtk doctor`, and the documented verification step all pass vacuously. Real errors are hidden.

```text
if !lf.is_empty() && entry.level.to_lowercase() != *lf {
```

### A318 Contract: subscribe() has no unsubscribe, so each resume adds duplicate handlers

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#336](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/336))
- Where: `core/api/src/lib.rs:226`, `core/api/src/lib.rs:708`, `core/kernel/main.rs:582`
- Problem: The contract has no unsubscribe. resume defaults to init, and switch_module calls resume on every enable.
- Impact: After repeated toggles, a module that subscribes in init has its handlers run once per earlier enable (which modules subscribe in init is not verified).
- Rule: `modules.idempotent` (docs/reference/modules.md)

```text
pub fn subscribe(event: &str, handler: impl Fn(Value) + Send + Sync + 'static) {
.push(Arc::new(handler));
fn resume(&self, ctx: ModuleContext) -> Result<(), String> {
module.resume(module_context(app, id, true))
```

### A319 Kernel: mp3-player enable state is saved even when its start fails

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#337](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/337))
- Where: `core/kernel/main.rs:599`, `core/kernel/main.rs:603`, `core/kernel/main.rs:205`
- Problem: For mp3-player the switch starts a thread and returns Ok, so a failed start is only logged, and the setting is saved afterwards. A read error after the switch leaves the module switched but not saved.
- Impact: After a restart the stored state can disagree with what ran in the session.
- Rule: `modules.lifecycle` (docs/reference/modules.md)

```text

let mut disabled = db.get_disabled_modules()?;
Err(error) => log_line(format!("setup: qbc music core bootstrap failed: {error}")),
```

### A320 Kernel: panic hook blocks on a full log queue and can re-enter the LOG_FILE lock

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#338](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/338))
- Where: `core/kernel/panic_hook.rs:24`, `core/kernel/logging.rs:185`, `core/kernel/logging.rs:39`, `core/kernel/logging.rs:256`
- Problem: The hook enqueues an ERROR entry with a blocking send. The queue keeps a receiver, so a full queue never disconnects. The hook then calls flush, which takes LOG_FILE.
- Impact: A panic that happens while LOG_FILE is held can hang that thread, and the crash log may never be written.
- Rule: `storage.log-queue` (docs/reference/storage.md)

```text
crate::logging::log_backend_at("ERROR", "panic", summary);
let _ = queue.tx.send(entry);
rx: Receiver<LogEntry>,
let Ok(mut guard) = slot.lock() else {
```

### A321 Kernel: flush() can return while the flusher is still writing its last batch

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#339](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/339))
- Where: `core/kernel/logging.rs:156`, `core/kernel/logging.rs:213`, `core/kernel/main.rs:164`
- Problem: flush drains only the channel. The flusher has already taken entries into a local batch and may still be writing them when flush returns.
- Impact: The last entries written before exit, possibly including ERROR lines, may be lost.
- Rule: `storage.log-queue` (docs/reference/storage.md)

```text
pub fn flush() {
write_batch(&batch);
crate::logging::flush();
```

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#340](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/340))
- Where: `core/kernel/main.rs:460`, `core/kernel/logging.rs:102`, `core/kernel/main.rs:77`
- Problem: Frontend entries have no length bound. unidtk.log grows by append with no rotation, and two file handles (main.rs and logging.rs) write to it.
- Impact: A single large message enlarges both the journal database and the text log without limit.
- Rule: `storage.log-queue` (docs/reference/storage.md)

```text

message: message.to_string(),
```

- Where: `core/kernel/events.rs:110`, `core/kernel/ws.rs:147`
- Problem: Live frames are dropped when a 64-slot subscriber channel is full. Only the resume paths send a gap frame.
- Impact: A slow WebView silently loses stream events such as message updates.

```text
let (sender, receiver) = crossbeam_channel::bounded::<crate::events::Frame>(64);
```

### A325 Kernel: forced wallpaper attach can clear the in-progress flag of a running attach

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#343](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/343))
- Where: `core/kernel/main.rs:1611`, `core/kernel/main.rs:1619`
- Problem: A forced attach never sets ATTACH_IN_PROGRESS, but its thread's drop guard clears the flag when it finishes.
- Impact: Two attach loops can run at once while a non-forced loop is still active.

- Where: `core/kernel/input.rs:117`, `core/kernel/input.rs:162`
- Problem: make_lparam does not mask the low value, so a negative x sets all high bits and the y value is replaced.
- Impact: Clicks forwarded at a negative client x arrive with wrong coordinates. The on-screen effect was not measured.


### A351 Kernel: every rejected WS attempt writes an INFO journal line

- Severity: medium. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#369](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/369))
- Where: `core/kernel/ws.rs:135`, `core/kernel/logging.rs:221`
- Problem: Each rejected attempt is logged at INFO, and the journal keeps 5000 entries before sweeping.
- Impact: Repeated attempts can push earlier journal entries out.

```text
crate::log_line("ws: connection rejected (missing or wrong token)");
```

### A352 Kernel: frontend text is written verbatim to unidtk.log, so newlines forge lines

- Severity: medium. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#370](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/370))
- Where: `core/kernel/logging.rs:269`, `core/kernel/main.rs:97`
- Problem: Message text is written without escaping, so an embedded newline starts a new line that looks like a journal entry.
- Impact: Forged lines can appear in unidtk.log.

```text
let _ = writeln!(file, "[{timestamp}] {prefix}{}", entry.message);
```

### A353 Kernel: each raw mouse report runs several Win32 hit-tests on the UI thread

- Severity: medium. Type: performance. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#371](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/371))
- Where: `core/kernel/input.rs:123`, `core/kernel/input.rs:319`, `core/kernel/input.rs:325`, `core/kernel/input.rs:102`
- Problem: Each raw mouse report calls WindowFromPoint, GetCursorPos, five GetAsyncKeyState calls, child hit-tests and PostMessageA on the UI thread. SetFocus runs for button presses.
- Impact: UI-thread load scales with the mouse polling rate. Visible lag was not measured.
- Rule: `kernel.no-blocking-main-thread` (docs/reference/kernel.md)

```text
let hwnd_under_cursor = WindowsAndMessaging::WindowFromPoint(point);
let current_wparam = get_current_mouse_wparam();
WindowsAndMessaging::WM_MOUSEMOVE,
```

### A354 Contract: global EMIT_ORDER mutex wraps the Tauri emit call and the event broker

- Severity: medium. Type: performance. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#372](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/372))
- Where: `core/api/src/lib.rs:207`, `core/api/src/lib.rs:215`, `core/kernel/main.rs:2362`
- Problem: All emits serialize on EMIT_ORDER, and the Tauri emit and the broker run inside that lock.
- Impact: A stalled emit would block every publisher. Whether the Tauri emit can block was not verified.

```text
let _order = EMIT_ORDER.lock().unwrap_or_else(|poisoned| poisoned.into_inner());
if let Some(emitter) = EMITTER.get() {
let _ = handle.emit(event, payload.clone());
```

### A355 Kernel: working set is emptied every 60 seconds and also exposed as a command

- Severity: medium. Type: performance. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#373](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/373))
- Where: `core/kernel/main.rs:2136`, `core/kernel/main.rs:1722`
- Problem: A background thread empties the process working set once a minute, and the same call is available as a UI command.
- Impact: Forced trims can add page faults to UI work. The size of this impact was not measured.

```text
std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_secs(60));
let _ = SetProcessWorkingSetSize(GetCurrentProcess(), usize::MAX, usize::MAX);
```

### A356 Kernel: cold start takes 7.5 to 10 seconds before the shell is ready

- Severity: medium. Type: performance. Status: reproduced on a live build. ([#374](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/374))
- Where: `core/kernel/main.rs:2344`, `core/kernel/main.rs:2372`, `core/kernel/main.rs:2404`
- Problem: Three consecutive starts in the journal show 7.5-10.3 s to enter setup, with the data store taking 0.5-4.1 s to open and the log store 0.5-3.9 s. Setup then joins both bootstrap threads on the main thread.
- Impact: After every logon the desktop stays empty for several seconds.
- Rule: `kernel.no-blocking-main-thread` (docs/reference/kernel.md)

```text
startup: Tauri entered setup at 10167 ms
startup: Tauri entered setup at 7548 ms
startup: Tauri entered setup at 10329 ms
```

### A357 Kernel: unwrap on socket clone and wallpaper mutex panics the thread on error

- Severity: low. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#375](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/375))
- Where: `core/kernel/ipc.rs:114`, `core/kernel/wallpaper.rs:449`, `core/kernel/wallpaper.rs:454`
- Problem: try_clone and the mutex lock use unwrap, so an error or a poisoned lock panics the connection or command thread.
- Impact: A panic ends that connection or command, and later calls depend on the same lock.

```text
let mut out = reader.get_mut().try_clone().unwrap();
return ORIGINAL_WALLPAPER.lock().unwrap().clone();
```

- Where: `core/kernel/events.rs:148`
- Problem: cursor + 1 is not checked, so a client cursor of u64::MAX wraps in release builds and panics in debug builds.
- Impact: The replay decision is wrong for that cursor value, or the call panics in debug builds.

```text
if cursor + 1 < oldest && !is_state_event(event) {
```

### A362 Contract: core/api hands the Tauri AppHandle to modules as an Any handle

- Severity: low. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#380](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/380))
- Where: `core/kernel/ipc.rs:614`, `core/api/src/commands.rs:145`, `core/api/src/lib.rs:6`
- Problem: The contract doc says it is free of Tauri, yet modules receive the AppHandle by downcast, and lib.rs:6-8 allows Tauri host types. lib.rs:3 says the contract has no tauri details.
- Impact: Modules can depend on Tauri despite the contract-only rule, even though core/api/Cargo.toml has no Tauri dependency.
- Rule: `kernel.stable-api` (docs/reference/kernel.md)

```text
let host = &server.app as &(dyn std::any::Any + Send + Sync);
/// The kernel passes its `tauri::AppHandle`, but the contract stays free of Tauri

```

### A363 Kernel: unused placeholder, empty branch, and duplicate APP_HANDLE and LOG_FILE statics

- Severity: low. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#381](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/381))
- Where: `core/kernel/main.rs:1561`, `core/kernel/hotkeys.rs:248`, `core/kernel/main.rs:71`, `core/kernel/hotkeys.rs:42`, `core/kernel/main.rs:75`, `core/kernel/logging.rs:44`
- Problem: _placeholder is an unused function, hotkeys.rs has an empty else branch, and APP_HANDLE and LOG_FILE are each defined in two modules.
- Impact: Duplicate globals hold separate copies of the same state.

```text
#[allow(dead_code)]
fn _placeholder() {}
static APP_HANDLE: OnceLock<AppHandle> = OnceLock::new();
static LOG_FILE: OnceLock<Mutex<Option<File>>> = OnceLock::new();
```

### A364 Repository: old project names remain in code, windows classes and file names

- Severity: low. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#382](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/382))
- Where: `core/kernel/shutdown.rs:75`, `core/kernel/shell_db.rs:1`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:6017`, `core/ui/harness.js:16`, `modules/mp3-player/rust/commands.rs:2`
- Problem: A grep across core, modules, opt/cli, toolchain and hosts finds the old names in code files: nothing/NothingWallpaper (9 files), shell-era names such as Shell_SessionEnd, __SHELL_ERRORS__, nothingShell and nothingExplorer (7 files), qbc-music (5), yt-music (3) and app-tauri (2). core/kernel/shell_db.rs is a legacy alias module.
- Impact: Names mislead readers and tools, and the project's own rule against compatibility aliases and old names is not met.

```text
let class_name: PCWSTR = w!("Shell_SessionEnd");
```

### A365 Kernel: the 1500 ms shutdown budget is repeated in six kernel sites and the contract

- Severity: low. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#383](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/383))
- Where: `core/kernel/main.rs:135`, `core/kernel/main.rs:138`, `core/kernel/main.rs:147`, `core/kernel/main.rs:153`, `core/kernel/main.rs:159`, `core/kernel/main.rs:192`, `core/api/src/lib.rs:704`
- Problem: The same 1500 ms literal appears in main.rs and as the default in the contract.
- Impact: Changing the shutdown budget means editing every copy.

```text
let _ = unidtk_api::Module::shutdown(&*module, Duration::from_millis(1500));
```

### A366 Kernel: WS token is compared with != and generated from RandomState hashers

- Severity: low. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#384](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/384))
- Where: `core/kernel/ws.rs:130`, `core/kernel/ws.rs:47`
- Problem: The token is compared with != rather than a constant-time comparison. It is generated from hashers rather than a documented CSPRNG.
- Impact: The comparison may leak timing information, which is hard to exploit over loopback.

```text
if presented.as_deref() != Some(token.as_str()) {
let mut first = std::collections::hash_map::RandomState::new().build_hasher();
```

### A367 Kernel: .env lookup walks six parent directories of the executable path

- Severity: low. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#385](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/385))
- Where: `core/kernel/main.rs:1732`, `core/kernel/main.rs:1733`
- Problem: Environment values are read from the first .env found up to six levels above the executable, and they are set when the variable is unset.
- Impact: A .env file in a parent folder can supply values, such as Telegram settings, to an installed copy.

```text
for _ in 0..6 {
let candidate = dir.join(".env");
```

### A368 Kernel: Telegram API credentials are compiled into the binary as fallback values

- Severity: low. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#386](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/386))
- Where: `core/kernel/main.rs:1758`, `core/kernel/main.rs:1755`
- Problem: Credentials fall back to build-time values compiled into the executable. The code comment says the values are the same for every user. The values are <redacted>.
- Impact: Anyone with the binary can read the embedded values.

```text
("TELEGRAM_API_ID", option_env!("TELEGRAM_API_ID")),
```

### A369 Kernel: global keyboard hook and raw-mouse sink run for the whole session

- Severity: low. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#387](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/387))
- Where: `core/kernel/hotkeys.rs:363`, `core/kernel/main.rs:2538`, `core/kernel/input.rs:677`
- Problem: The keyboard hook starts at setup and sees every keystroke of the session, and the raw mouse device sink is registered at the same time. No keystroke logging was seen in these files.
- Impact: Input for other applications passes through this process for the whole session.

```text
let hook = match SetWindowsHookExW(WH_KEYBOARD_LL, Some(keyboard_hook_proc), h_instance, 0) {
hotkeys::init(app.handle().clone());
dwFlags: Input::RIDEV_INPUTSINK,
```

### A370 Kernel: per-connection subscription list grows with no size cap

- Severity: low. Type: performance. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#388](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/388))
- Where: `core/kernel/ws.rs:235`
- Problem: Each subscribe frame with a new event name appends to a Vec with no limit.
- Impact: A single connection can grow kernel memory without bound.

```text
subscribed.push(event.clone());
```

- Severity: low. Type: documentation. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#389](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/389))

```text
pub fn ensure_cli_in_path() -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
| `HKCU\Environment\Path` | `core/kernel/path_env.rs`, run by `udtk path ensure` |
```

## Core UI (desk, menu, theme)

### A372 Core UI: names concrete modules in about 274 lines of unidtk-core.js, so the core is not module-agnostic

- Severity: high. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#390](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/390))
- Where: `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:373`, `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:587`, `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:856`, `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:1745`, `core/ui/widgets-api.js:67`
- Problem: unidtk-core.js has 274 lines mentioning telegram, mp3-player, clock, glyph-bar, micrographics, vpn or explorer (menu.js 32, widgets-api.js 26, theme.js 14, index.html 11). Examples: a built-in special-type table, an if-chain that imports each module's widget, widget loops started by name, a wheel handler that knows module CSS classes, a fallback module list in widgets-api.js, and a clock morph of about 200 lines.
- Impact: The core cannot be built or reasoned about without the modules, and a new module needs edits in the core.
- Rule: `kernel.core-invisible` (docs/reference/kernel.md)

```text
if (w.specialType === "clock") {
  ...
} else if (w.specialType === "mp3-player") {
  ...
} else if (w.specialType === "glyph-bar") {
```

### A373 Core UI: adding a module needs edits in about ten places of the core instead of one metadata folder

- Severity: high. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#391](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/391))
- Where: `core/ui/index.html:25`, `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:373`, `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:587`, `core/ui/widgets-api.js:67`, `core/kernel/main.rs:593`, `core/kernel/main.rs:124`, `core/kernel/ipc.rs:594`, `core/kernel/registry.rs:12`
- Problem: A module needs: a stylesheet link in index.html, an entry in the special-type table and if-chain of unidtk-core.js, the fallback list in widgets-api.js, the match in apply_module_enabled, a step in app_shutdown, a route in the socket table, two lists in registry.rs and a dependency line in toolchain/Cargo.toml. The build lint checks only some of these.
- Impact: The vertical-slice promise does not hold: modules cannot be added or removed without touching the core, and forgotten places fail silently.
- Rule: `modules.vertical-slice` (docs/reference/modules.md)

```text
<link rel="stylesheet" href="../modules/clock/clock.css" />
```

### A374 Menu: "From Start menu…" in a desk folder opens a view that does not exist and leaves the panel blank

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: reproduced on a live build. ([#392](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/392))
- Where: `core/ui/menu.js:169`, `core/ui/menu.js:143`, `core/ui/folder-body.js:159`
- Problem: showView only knows desk, look and vpn. After the view was renamed, openFoldersFor still sets currentView to "folders", so every pane is hidden. On the live build the panel showed only the header and footer; reopening the menu kept it blank until a tab was clicked.
- Impact: A visible context-menu action opens an empty settings panel, and the start-menu picker never appears.

```text
export function openFoldersFor(folderId) {
  startPickerFor = folderId;
  currentView = "folders";
```

### A375 Desk: core code calls nudgeSystemVolume, updateClockTime and telegramRuntime that are not defined in core

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#393](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/393)) Fixed in `c5e17cd`.
- Where: `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:1791`, `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:1831`, `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:2488`, `modules/glyph-bar/ui/widget.js:235`, `modules/clock/ui/widget.js:176`
- Problem: These identifiers are defined only inside the glyph-bar, clock and telegram modules and are not imported into unidtk-core.js. None of them exists on `window` either (checked live). The first is reached from the wallpaper-wheel handler, the second is hidden by try/catch, the third from the telegram-send-file handler. A ReferenceError at runtime was not reproduced because a synthetic wheel event was not delivered.
- Impact: Mouse wheel over the volume bar in wallpaper mode, the clock refresh on becoming visible, and "Send to Telegram" from the explorer are likely broken or silent.
- Rule: `modules.no-module-to-module` (docs/reference/modules.md)

```text
if (notches) nudgeSystemVolume(notches * 10);
try { updateClockTime(); } catch {}
if (!telegramRuntime.chatsLoaded && !telegramRuntime.chats?.length) {
```

### A376 Toast: a new toast dismisses the previous one together with its Undo action

- Severity: medium. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#394](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/394))
- Where: `core/ui/toast.js:44`, `core/ui/folders.js:84`, `core/ui/menu.js:372`
- Problem: Only one toast exists at a time. Showing any new toast removes the current one immediately, even if it carries an Undo button within its 5 s window. Deleting a folder or shortcut and then triggering any other toast discards the first Undo.
- Impact: The project's rule that irreversible actions are undoable for 5 seconds can be defeated by an unrelated message.
- Rule: `desk.no-blocking-dialogs` (docs/reference/desk.md)

```text
export function showToast(message, { tone = "info", ... } = {}) {
  dismiss(current);
```

### A377 Desk: shortcut icons come from a hardcoded table of 37 names, including personal servers and games

- Severity: medium. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#395](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/395))
- Where: `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:1366`, `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:1372`, `core/ui/folder-body.js:78`, `core/ui/shortcut-icons.js:1`
- Problem: getShortcutIcon maps shortcut names ("сервер - rdp", "blender 5.2", "how to fish", "tlauncher", ...) to hand-drawn SVGs, with a small file-type fallback. The settings menu already shows the real Windows icon through shortcut_icon, so the two views disagree.
- Impact: Unlisted shortcuts get a generic glyph on the desk, personal entries are baked into the product, and the table is about 240 lines of core code.
- Rule: `theme.tokens-only` (docs/reference/desk.md)

### A378 Desk: default widget sizes are defined in three places and disagree

- Severity: medium. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#396](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/396))
- Where: `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:149`, `core/ui/theme.js:60`, `core/kernel/main.rs:518`, `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:1258`, `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:1980`, `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:1841`
- Problem: The clock is 210x180 in defaultWidgetPosition, 207x187 in theme.js PRESET_GEOMETRY and 210x190 in the kernel default layout; telegram and glyph-bar differ too. Other literals in the same file: the expanded card is 400 wide and at most 500 high, resize is capped at 800 px, snap magnet 10 px and gap 12 px, a cascade offset of 88 px, and a 340x64 fallback size.
- Impact: Which size a widget gets depends on which path created it, and none of these limits is a setting or a token.

```text
const sizes = {
  clock: { w: 210, h: 180 },
  "mp3-player": { w: 210, h: 220 },
```

### A379 Menu: six "Настроить…" buttons have identical accessible names, and two modules open a sheet that is only an on/off s...

- Severity: medium. Type: accessibility. Status: reproduced on a live build. ([#397](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/397))
- Where: `core/ui/menu.js:325`, `core/ui/menu.js:252`, `core/ui/menu.js:261`
- Problem: On the live Desk tab the semantic tree lists six buttons named "Настроить…" with no module name, and three more for folders. For modules without a settings view (clock), the sheet only offers "Включить / Скрыть" and the description "Точечные часы Nothing"; the button label says configure but the action is a state switch.
- Impact: Screen-reader users cannot tell the buttons apart, and sighted users expect settings and find only a toggle.

```text
<button type="button" class="menu-btn" data-widget-open>Настроить…</button>
```

### A380 Frontend log: widget render failures are logged at INFO, so the ERROR audit cannot see them

- Severity: medium. Type: tooling. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#398](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/398))
- Where: `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:605`, `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:623`, `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:734`, `docs/dev/logging.md:56`, `core/kernel/logging.rs:102`
- Problem: frontend_log accepts a level, but these failure paths pass none, so they are stored as INFO. Only boot-log.js passes "ERROR". docs/dev/logging.md:56 and :232 still say a frontend line is always INFO, while the kernel now honours the level.
- Impact: A widget that fails to render does not appear in `udtk logs --level ERROR` or in the doctor audit, and the documentation describes a behaviour that no longer exists.

```text
invoke("frontend_log", { message: `MUSIC RENDER FAIL: ${w.id}: ${err?.stack || err?.message || err}` }).catch(() => {});
```

### A381 Frontend log: every rlog/flog line is written to the journal twice

- Severity: low. Type: bug. Status: reproduced on a live build. ([#399](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/399))
- Where: `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:15`, `core/ui/widgets-api.js:26`, `core/ui/boot-log.js:7`
- Problem: rlog and flog call frontend_log directly and then call window.__wallpaper_log, which calls frontend_log again. In the live journal every "[bridge] moduleIds" and "[bridge] loaded widgets" line appears twice with consecutive ids. getWidgets also re-reads module metadata from disk and logs on every call.
- Impact: The 5000-entry journal fills twice as fast and evicts real history sooner.

```text
function rlog(msg) { console.log(msg); invoke("frontend_log", { message: msg }).catch(()=>{}); try{ window.__wallpaper_log?.(msg);}catch{} }
```

### A382 Desk: uncaught errors are shown to the user as raw text for four seconds

- Severity: low. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#400](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/400))
- Where: `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:1819`, `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:1820`, `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:1722`
- Problem: Every window error and unhandled rejection is printed in a debug strip with the raw message, and cleared after 4 seconds. The text is a developer message, mixes the error text into a Russian label, and is not kept anywhere the user can find it again.
- Impact: Users see cryptic technical text that disappears before it can be read or reported.

```text
window.addEventListener("unhandledrejection", (e) => showDebugMessage(`Необработанный промис: ${e.reason}`));
```

### A383 Menu: the Look tab renders 279 controls, including 22 tint rows with eight identically named dots each

- Severity: low. Type: ux. Status: reproduced on a live build. ([#401](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/401))
- Where: `core/ui/menu.js:782`, `core/ui/theme.js:88`

"Цвет 6", "Коралловый", repeated per target. The side panel also covers the right edge of the desk, so the Telegram dock and volume bar being tinted are hidden while editing.

### A385 Core UI: dead code remains in unidtk-core.js (unused caches, renderer registry, helpers)
