
### A386 Cross-module: metadata.json omits contract keys that modules.md lists

- Severity: low. Type: architecture. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#404](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/404))
- Where: `modules/vpn/ui/metadata.json:23`, `modules/micrographics/ui/metadata.json:118`, `modules/glyph-bar/ui/metadata.json:18`, `modules/clock/ui/metadata.json:11`
- Problem: VPN lacks lookOptions, container and motion.open. Micrographics lacks container, embeddable, settingsView and motion.open. Glyph bar lacks settingsView, lookOptions, container, embeddable and motion.open. Clock lacks settingsView, lookOptions and embeddable.
- Impact: The menu and desk may not treat these modules as the documented contract describes. Whether the build lint enforces these keys was not checked.
- Rule: `modules.metadata` (docs/reference/modules.md)

```text
"settingsView": "vpn"
"backend": true
"open": ""
```

### A387 Core UI: literal millisecond values for toasts, debug message, menu close and display debounce

- Severity: low. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#405](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/405))
- Where: `core/ui/toast.js:11`, `core/ui/toast.js:78`, `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:1727`, `core/ui/menu.js:189`, `core/ui/unidtk-core.js:2588`
- Problem: Toast lifetime 5000/7000 ms, debug message 4000 ms, menu-close fallback 700 ms and display-change debounce 150 ms are literals outside the motion tokens. The 5 s Undo window cannot be changed by the user.
- Impact: Timing does not follow the motion settings or reduced-motion, and users who need longer to read or undo cannot extend it.
- Rule: `motion.no-hardcoded-ms` (docs/reference/motion.md)

```text
const UNDO_MS = 5000;
...
entry.timer = setTimeout(() => dismiss(entry), tone === "error" ? Math.max(timeout, 7000) : timeout);
```

### A388 Menu: fonts and palette offer fixed short lists; users cannot add their own font or more than 8 palette slots

- Severity: low. Type: customization. Status: reproduced on a live build. ([#406](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/406))
- Where: `core/ui/theme.js:68`, `core/ui/theme.js:88`, `core/ui/theme.js:258`
- Problem: Display, body and mono fonts are validated against fixed sets of 3-4 stacks, and the live menu shows four font pills (Geist, Segoe UI, Системный, Ndot). The palette is a fixed list of 8 named slots.
- Impact: A user cannot choose an installed font or extend the palette; any other value is rejected by normalizeTheme.

```text
export const PALETTE_SLOTS = ["neutral", "blue", "aqua", "mint", "lilac", "peach", "magenta", "coral"];
```

## CLI (`udtk`)

### A389 CLI: gui js: runs arbitrary page JavaScript through the unauthenticated CDP port

- Severity: high. Type: security. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#407](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/407))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:782`, `opt/cli/main.rs:201`, `core/kernel/command_inventory.rs:52`
- Problem: The CDP port is published in the unauthenticated ping reply. The gui js: step evaluates any expression in the WebView through that port.
- Impact: Any local process can call the UI write commands through CDP, bypassing the socket read-only policy.
- Rule: `ipc.socket-read-only` (docs/reference/ipc.md)

```text
"js" => match cdp.evaluate(body) {
```

### A390 CLI: udtk gui --json exits 0 when a step failed

- Severity: high. Type: tooling. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#408](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/408)) Fixed in `517280b`.
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:919`, `opt/cli/main.rs:990`
- Problem: The JSON branch returns Ok before the failure check at line 990. The exit status is 0 while the body reports ok false.
- Impact: Scripts and CI that read the exit code treat a failed UI step as a pass.

```text
if json_flag {
return Ok(serde_json::to_string_pretty(&serde_json::json!({
"ok": !failed,
```

### A391 CLI: doctor -simulate motion budget reports OK when the probe read fails

- Severity: high. Type: tooling. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#409](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/409)) Fixed in `517280b`.
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:3610`, `opt/cli/main.rs:3629`
- Problem: A failed probe read becomes a null value, and phase() maps null to zero frame and task times. The verdict then compares zeros with the budget.
- Impact: The motion budget check cannot fail when its probe read fails.

```text
let open = eval(&mut cdp, "readMotionProbe()").unwrap_or_default();
```

### A392 CLI: CDP HTTP reader stops at the header terminator before the body arrives

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#410](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/410))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:224`
- Problem: The read loop ends once the header separator is seen and does not check that the body is complete. The JSON body can arrive in a later segment.
- Impact: CDP queries can fail intermittently with a bad CDP json error in doctor, shot and gui.

```text
if resp.contains(CRLF.repeat(2).as_str()) || resp.len() > 16 * 1024 {
break;
```

### A393 CLI: CDP port falls back to 9222 when the ping reply omits cdpPort

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#411](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/411))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:205`, `opt/cli/main.rs:35`
- Problem: A ping reply without cdpPort makes the CLI use the conventional port 9222. The CLI does not confirm the owner of that port at this step.
- Impact: The CLI can connect to a foreign or stale WebView listening on 9222.
- Rule: `ipc.cdp-always-on` (docs/reference/ipc.md)

```text
None => Ok(CDP_FALLBACK),
```

### A394 CLI: udtk stop reports stopped although taskkill results are discarded

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#412](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/412))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:1667`, `opt/cli/main.rs:1684`
- Problem: The taskkill exit status is ignored. The command returns stopped unconditionally after the wait loop.
- Impact: udtk build continues to replace files while processes may still hold them.

```text
let _ = std::process::Command::new("taskkill")
.args(["/PID", &pid.to_string(), "/F"])
```

### A395 CLI: udtk stop reports success once the quit call is dispatched

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#413](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/413))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:1651`, `opt/cli/main.rs:1658`
- Problem: The graceful branch sets its flag when the quit call is sent and does not wait for the app to exit. A fixed grace period is followed by forced termination.
- Impact: A quit that does not complete is reported as graceful, and the app is terminated after the fixed delay.

```text
"(() => { const h = window.__UNIDTK_HARNESS__; if (!h) return false; h.invoke('quit_app').catch(() => {}); return true; })()",
```

### A396 CLI: udtk build restores its own binary only when cargo fails

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#414](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/414))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:1425`, `opt/cli/main.rs:1430`
- Problem: The rename of the running udtk.exe discards its error. The restore runs only when cargo fails, not after later failures or an interruption.
- Impact: A failed or interrupted build can leave target\release without a runnable udtk.exe.

```text
let _ = std::fs::rename(&exe, &held);
let _ = std::fs::rename(&held, &exe);
```

### A397 CLI: installer lookup takes the first *-setup.exe in the NSIS output folder

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#415](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/415))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:1532`
- Problem: The first matching file in directory order is used, and older installers stay in the output folder. Which file is chosen is unverified.
- Impact: The installer run may not be the one just built, and the size check then waits 60 seconds before failing.

```text
name.ends_with("-setup.exe") && p.is_file()
```

### A398 CLI: path status reports the process PATH as the User PATH

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#416](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/416))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:2216`, `opt/cli/main.rs:2221`
- Problem: The status check reads the current process environment. Its output names the User PATH.
- Impact: Status can report the directory as present while the registry User PATH lacks it.

```text
let path_var = std::env::var("PATH").unwrap_or_default();
```

### A399 CLI: path ensure matches substrings and sends no environment-change broadcast

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#417](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/417))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:2195`
- Problem: The check is a wildcard substring match, unlike the exact-entry check in status. No environment-change broadcast is sent. Whether the write changes REG_EXPAND_SZ to REG_SZ is unverified.
- Impact: A longer entry containing the directory suppresses the add, and running processes keep the old PATH until re-login.

```text
if ($old -notlike \"*$dir*\") {{ [Environment]::SetEnvironmentVariable('Path', \"$old;$dir\", 'User') }}
```

### A400 CLI: console helper processes spawned without the no-window creation flag

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#418](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/418))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:1978`, `opt/cli/main.rs:2031`, `opt/cli/main.rs:2198`, `opt/cli/main.rs:2255`, `opt/cli/main.rs:2719`
- Problem: schtasks, powershell.exe, powershell and cargo check are spawned without creation_flags(NO_WINDOW). The probe at line 2719 uses a literal 0x08000000.
- Impact: When the CLI runs from the installer hook or a GUI parent, a console window can appear.
- Rule: `install.native-tasks` (docs/reference/install.md)

```text
std::process::Command::new("schtasks")
.args(["/query", "/tn", task_name])
```

### A401 CLI: tasks status and path status exit 0 when an item is missing

- Severity: medium. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#419](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/419))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:2075`, `opt/cli/main.rs:2092`, `opt/cli/main.rs:2220`
- Problem: Both commands return Ok after printing MISSING or NOT in PATH.
- Impact: Scripts cannot detect a missing autostart task or PATH entry from the exit code.

```text
let state_str = if reg { "OK (Registered)" } else { "MISSING" };
```

### A402 CLI: logs falls back to defaults silently for bad or missing flag values

- Severity: medium. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#420](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/420))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:155`, `opt/cli/main.rs:160`, `opt/cli/main.rs:165`
- Problem: A non-numeric limit becomes 50 and a bad since value becomes none. A --level or --source flag without a value is dropped.
- Impact: udtk logs returns a different record set than requested, with no message.

```text
limit = v.parse().unwrap_or(50);
```

### A403 CLI: tasks, autostart, path, doctor, build and shot accept duplicate spellings

- Severity: medium. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#421](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/421))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:2070`, `opt/cli/main.rs:2180`, `opt/cli/main.rs:2278`, `opt/cli/main.rs:1716`, `opt/cli/main.rs:1114`
- Problem: Several commands accept a second name for one action, for example list for status and on for enable. Doctor accepts both -ping and --ping.
- Impact: The help text lists only one spelling, so the accepted surface is larger than documented.

```text
"status" | "list" => {
"enable" | "on" => {
```

### A404 CLI: udtk doc builds the data cache path itself instead of the paths module

- Severity: medium. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#422](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/422))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:1883`
- Problem: The CLI calls dirs directly and joins UniDTK and cache itself. unidtk_api::paths::cache_dir() provides the same location.
- Impact: A change to the data layout must be made in the CLI as well as in the paths module.
- Rule: `install.paths-in-one-place` (docs/reference/install.md)

```text
let output = dirs::data_local_dir()
.ok_or("no local data directory")?
.join("UniDTK")
.join("cache")
```

### A405 CLI: program directory literal and a second install-dir definition in the CLI

- Severity: medium. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#423](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/423))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:1949`, `opt/cli/main.rs:1946`
- Problem: program_files_install_dir hard-codes C:\Program Files as a fallback and joins UniDTK. It is a second definition beside unidtk_api::paths::install_dir.
- Impact: Install-location knowledge lives in two places.
- Rule: `install.paths-in-one-place` (docs/reference/install.md)

```text
.unwrap_or_else(|_| "C:\\Program Files".into());
```

### A406 CLI: command names are sent as literals instead of inventory constants

- Severity: medium. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#424](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/424))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:486`, `opt/cli/main.rs:496`, `opt/cli/main.rs:1651`
- Problem: log_max_id and log_delta are sent as string literals although the inventory declares them. quit_app, used by stop, is absent from the inventory.
- Impact: A rename in the inventory does not reach these call sites, and the UI quit command is undeclared.
- Rule: `ipc.one-contract` (docs/reference/ipc.md)

```text
ipc(serde_json::json!({"cmd": "log_max_id"}))
```

### A407 CLI: udtk compiles every module crate and runs module init in the CLI process

- Severity: medium. Type: architecture. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#425](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/425))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:26`, `core/kernel/registry.rs:12`, `toolchain/Cargo.toml:15`
- Problem: The udtk binary includes the kernel registry, which constructs all module crates. doctor -modules runs module init inside the CLI process.
- Impact: The CLI cannot be built without the full module graph, and init side effects in the CLI are not measured.

```text
#[path = "../../core/kernel/registry.rs"]
mod registry;
```

### A408 CLI: literal user name admin and absolute paths in code, task XML and scripts

- Severity: medium. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#426](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/426))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:1968`, `scheduler-tasks/UniDTKSingboxTask.xml:14`, `scheduler-tasks/UniDTKVpnStopTask.xml:13`, `opt/libmpv/fetch-libmpv.ps1:19`, `opt/cli/main.rs:3831`
- Problem: The account name admin is a literal in code and in two task templates. Absolute paths appear in a PowerShell script and in a test.
- Impact: Task registration for another account depends on a USERNAME comparison, and the script fails if 7-Zip is installed elsewhere.
- Rule: `install.no-absolute-paths` (docs/reference/install.md)

```text
let current_user = std::env::var("USERNAME").unwrap_or_else(|_| "admin".into());
```

### A409 CLI: IPC, CDP and telegram proxy ports are literals in several files

- Severity: medium. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#427](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/427))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:32`, `opt/cli/main.rs:35`, `toolchain/tauri.conf.json:25`, `hosts/gpui/src/widgets/top_dashboard.rs:260`, `modules/telegram/rust/telegram.rs:74`
- Problem: Port 26540 appears as a literal in the CLI, the CSP and the GPUI host. The CDP fallback 9222 and the telegram proxy port 12909 are literals too.
- Impact: A port change must be made in each file, and a missed file silently disagrees.

```text
const IPC: &str = "127.0.0.1:26540";
```

### A410 CLI: timeouts, sleeps and retry counts are fixed literals, some repeated

- Severity: medium. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#428](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/428))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:92`, `opt/cli/main.rs:306`, `opt/cli/main.rs:1499`, `opt/cli/main.rs:2949`, `opt/cli/main.rs:3107`
- Problem: Connect, read, write, CDP, install, probe and ping timeouts are literals in many functions. The 8-second and 10-second CDP deadlines and the 2000 ms init message are repeated.
- Impact: Timeouts cannot be changed without editing several functions, and message text can disagree with the value.

```text
TcpStream::connect_timeout(&socket_addr, Duration::from_millis(500))
```

### A411 CLI: install directory, ports, screenshot folder and log limit have no override

- Severity: medium. Type: customization. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#429](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/429))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:149`, `opt/cli/main.rs:1947`, `opt/cli/main.rs:1077`, `opt/cli/main.rs:1718`
- Problem: The install directory, IPC and CDP ports, screenshot folder and log limit cannot be set by flag or environment. The build thread count is fixed in .cargo/config.toml, and -j has no upper bound.
- Impact: Machine-specific settings require source edits, and a very large -j value is accepted.

```text
let mut limit = 50u64;
```

### A412 CLI: process stop matches executable paths by prefix without a separator

- Severity: medium. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#430](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/430))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:1692`
- Problem: The install directory is compared as a plain prefix with no trailing separator. Sibling directories sharing the prefix also match.
- Impact: taskkill /F can terminate processes that run from unrelated directories.

```text
$_.ExecutablePath.ToLower().StartsWith('{}')
```

### A413 CLI: udtk run and cargo calls load .env values and search parent directories

- Severity: medium. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#431](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/431))

- Impact: A .env file in a parent directory can supply the Telegram credentials at runtime and in builds.

```text
for _ in 0..3 {
let candidate = dir.join(".env");
```

### A420 CLI: `udtk doctor -env` always fails on an installed copy because it looks for a repository .env

- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:1911`, `opt/cli/main.rs:2517`

```text
out.push_str("  .env: MISSING (.env not found at the repository root)\n");
failures += 1;
```


### A426 CLI: build, gui, shot and doctor silently accept unknown arguments

- Severity: low. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#444](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/444))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:1411`, `opt/cli/main.rs:753`, `opt/cli/main.rs:1131`, `opt/cli/main.rs:3171`
- Problem: Unknown flags and extra words are dropped without an error. Doctor validates only words that start with a dash.
- Impact: A misspelled flag has no effect and the command runs with its defaults.

```text
let (jobs, _rest, parse) = parse_build_jobs(args);
```

- Severity: low. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#445](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/445))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:1223`, `opt/cli/main.rs:1195`
- Problem: The JSON form exists only on success. Errors return plain text on stderr.
- Impact: Scripts that parse --json output cannot parse failures.

```text
if json_flag {
return Ok(serde_json::to_string_pretty(&serde_json::json!({
"ok": true,
```

### A428 CLI: bare udtk switches the parent console to UTF-8 and never restores it

- Severity: low. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#446](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/446))
- Where: `opt/cli/splash.rs:504`
- Problem: The splash screen sets the output code page of the console it runs in and does not restore it on exit.
- Impact: The invoking shell keeps code page 65001 after udtk exits.

```text
let cp = SetConsoleOutputCP(65001) == 1;
```

### A429 CLI: two CDP WebSocket clients use different frame limits and deadlines

- Severity: low. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#447](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/447))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:310`, `opt/cli/main.rs:370`
- Problem: cdp_call and Cdp::open each build a WebSocket with their own frame limit and deadline. The two copies of the connect logic can diverge.
- Impact: Callers get different limits and timeouts for the same protocol.

```text
ws_config.max_frame_size = Some(64 * 1024 * 1024);
config.max_frame_size = Some(16 * 1024 * 1024);
```

- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:2240`, `opt/cli/main.rs:1786`
- Problem: find_toolchain searches four parent levels, and run_cargo_check repeats the search with six.
- Impact: The two commands can find different toolchain directories.

```text
for _ in 0..6 {
let candidate = dir.join("toolchain");
```

- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:3643`, `core/kernel/registry.rs:23`
- Problem: base64_encode is used only by tests. ALL_MODULE_IDS in registry.rs has no code reader beyond build-script error text.
- Impact: Unused code is compiled into the shipped binaries.

```text
#[allow(dead_code)]
fn base64_encode(data: &[u8]) -> String {
```

### A432 CLI: process, task, file and tool names are literals across the CLI

- Severity: low. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#450](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/450))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:1484`, `opt/cli/main.rs:1632`, `opt/cli/main.rs:1463`, `opt/cli/main.rs:1861`, `opt/cli/main.rs:272`, `opt/cli/main.rs:2198`
- Problem: The task name UniDTK, the binary and DLL names, the model path and file names, the legacy task names, the Tauri origin and the powershell name are literals.
- Impact: Renames of these items must be applied at each call site.

```text
.args(["/run", "/tn", "UniDTK"])
```

### A433 CLI: splash falls back to a hard-coded 100 by 24 terminal size

- Severity: low. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#451](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/451))
- Where: `opt/cli/splash.rs:515`
- Problem: When no console size is available, the layout uses 100 columns and 24 rows.
- Impact: Output for redirected or unusual hosts is laid out for a fixed size.

```text
(false, 100, 24)
```

### A434 CLI: rendered book links hardcode the GitHub owner and branch

- Severity: low. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#452](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/452))
- Where: `opt/cli/book.rs:7`
- Problem: The repository URL is a literal, and the book tests repeat it.
- Impact: Links from a copy of this repository point at the original owner's repository.

```text
const REPOSITORY: &str = "https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/blob/main";
```

### A435 CLI: untracked scratch folder holds personal user-profile and Program Files paths

- Severity: low. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#453](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/453)) Closed as not planned: the files are local, untracked and gitignored, so they are not part of the repository.
- Where: 
- Problem: Loose scripts in scratch/ name a personal profile path and the Program Files install path.
- Impact: The scripts run only on the author's machine layout. The folder is gitignored, so it is not committed.

### A436 CLI: elevated task XML is staged at a fixed path in the user temp directory

- Severity: low. Type: security. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#454](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/454))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:2008`, `opt/cli/main.rs:2013`
- Problem: The XML file has a predictable name in the user-writable temp directory and is read later by an elevated schtasks /create call.
- Impact: A same-user process can replace the file between the write and the elevated read.

```text
let temp_xml = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("unidtk_task_{}.xml", def.name));
```

### A437 CLI: 347 MB model is copied on every udtk build with no unchanged check

- Severity: low. Type: performance. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#455](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/455))
- Where: `opt/cli/main.rs:1466`
- Problem: The model copy runs on every build. The DLL copy in the same function has an unchanged check, and the model copy has none.
- Impact: Each build rewrites about 347 MB of model data in target/release.

```text
std::fs::copy(&src, target_model_dir.join(file))
```

### A438 CLI: splash command map omits doc and duplicates the hand-written help lists

- Severity: low. Type: documentation. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#456](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/456))
- Where: `opt/cli/splash.rs:52`, `opt/cli/main.rs:1275`, `opt/cli/main.rs:3666`
- Problem: The splash COMMANDS array has no doc entry. help() and cli_commands() are two hand-written lists of the same commands.
- Impact: The splash, help text and manifest can drift apart, and the splash already omits doc.

```text
("system", "tasks · path · autostart · build · stop · run"),
```

### A439 CLI: splash_art header names a make_splash.py generator absent from the repo

- Severity: low. Type: documentation. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#457](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/457))
- Where: `opt/cli/splash_art.rs:3`
- Problem: The generated art file names make_splash.py as its generator. No such file exists in the repository outside target/.
- Impact: The emblem cannot be regenerated from the repository as the header describes.

```text
//! Regenerate with `make_splash.py` (docs/dev/ascii-art.md, "Recorded Variants"):
```

## Build, installer, scheduled tasks

### A440 Installer: pre-install hook deletes module folders before the running-app check

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#458](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/458))
- Where: `toolchain/windows/hooks.nsh:26`, `toolchain/windows/hooks.nsh:25`
- Problem: PREINSTALL removes the modules and fonts folders. Its own comment says this runs before CheckIfAppIsRunning.
- Impact: A cancelled install or an upgrade of a running app leaves the installed app without its modules.

```text
RMDir /r "$INSTDIR\modules"
RMDir /r "$INSTDIR\fonts"
```

### A441 Installer: opener scope allows opening any path from the page

- Severity: high. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#459](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/459))
- Where: `toolchain/tauri.conf.json:80`
- Problem: The opener:allow-open-path permission lists the glob ** in its allow scope. The scope is not restricted to the app's own directories.
- Impact: The page can ask the OS shell to open any path, including executables.

```text
"path": "**"
```

### A442 Installer: asset protocol scope covers the whole filesystem

- Severity: high. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#460](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/460))
- Where: `toolchain/tauri.conf.json:117`
- Problem: The asset protocol scope lists the glob ** and the $HOME tree. The scope is not limited to app data.
- Impact: The page can read any file on the machine through the asset protocol.

```text
"scope": [
"**",
```

### A443 Installer: uninstall removes tasks but leaves the install directory in User PATH

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#461](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/461))
- Where: `toolchain/windows/hooks.nsh:58`
- Problem: PREUNINSTALL unregisters the scheduled tasks only. Neither the hooks nor the CLI remove the install directory from the User PATH.
- Impact: The User PATH keeps a directory that no longer exists after uninstall.

```text
nsExec::ExecToStack '"$INSTDIR\udtk.exe" tasks unregister all'
```

### A444 Build: copy failures of binaries and frontend files are discarded by build.rs

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#462](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/462))
- Where: `toolchain/build.rs:204`, `toolchain/build.rs:91`
- Problem: build.rs ignores copy errors for native DLLs and executables and for frontend assets. Stale copies remain without a message.
- Impact: A locked file leaves a stale binary in target/release, which the installer then packs.

```text
let _ = std::fs::copy(&path, &dest);
```

### A445 Scheduled tasks: autostart task allows parallel instances and sets no restart

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#463](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/463))
- Where: `scheduler-tasks/UniDTKTask.xml:24`
- Problem: MultipleInstancesPolicy is Parallel. The XML has no RestartOnFailure element.
- Impact: Each start request runs another instance, and a crashed shell stays down until the next logon.

```text
<MultipleInstancesPolicy>Parallel</MultipleInstancesPolicy>
```

### A446 Build: root holds Cargo.toml, Cargo.lock, .cargo and target outside toolchain

- Severity: medium. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#464](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/464))
- Where: `Cargo.toml:1`, `.cargo/config.toml:1`
- Problem: The workspace manifest, lockfile, cargo config and target directory are at the repository root. toolchain/Cargo.lock is a second lockfile that cargo does not read.
- Impact: Two lockfiles exist, and the one in toolchain/ is stale.
- Rule: `repo.flat-root` (docs/reference/kernel.md)

```text
# Single Cargo workspace: one `Cargo.lock`, one `target/`.
```

### A447 Build: native binaries are copied into a directory derived from OUT_DIR ancestors

- Severity: medium. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#465](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/465))
- Where: `toolchain/build.rs:545`
- Problem: The build script writes binaries into the profile directory computed from OUT_DIR ancestors, not into OUT_DIR.
- Impact: Build outputs are written outside the directory Cargo tracks for this build script.

```text
if let Some(target_dir) = out_path.ancestors().nth(3) {
```

### A448 Build: every .env key is embedded into all binaries as a compile-time variable

- Severity: medium. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#466](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/466))
- Where: `toolchain/build.rs:528`, `toolchain/build.rs:506`
- Problem: build.rs emits each .env key as a cargo:rustc-env value. Its comment says there is no secret to protect, while the .env header says not to share the file.
- Impact: Telegram API credentials are present in the shipped binaries and the installer.

```text
println!("cargo:rustc-env={key}={value}");
```

### A449 Installer: SHA-256 values pinned in READMEs are not checked by any step

- Severity: medium. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#467](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/467))
- Where: `opt/singbox/README.md:9`, `opt/byedpi/README.md:11`
- Problem: The singbox and byedpi READMEs record SHA-256 values for their binaries. No build, doctor or installer step compares them.
- Impact: A replaced binary with the same file name passes every check.

### A450 Installer: libmpv download script copies the DLL without a hash check

- Severity: medium. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#468](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/468))
- Where: `opt/libmpv/fetch-libmpv.ps1:16`
- Problem: The script downloads a GitHub release archive and extracts libmpv-2.dll. It compares no hash before extraction.
- Impact: The DLL bundled into the installer is not verified against any pinned value.

```text
Invoke-WebRequest -Uri $url -OutFile $archive
```

### A451 Installer: CSP allows WebSocket connections to every local port

- Severity: medium. Type: security. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#469](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/469))
- Where: `toolchain/tauri.conf.json:25`
- Problem: connect-src includes the wildcard port ws://127.0.0.1:*. It also names port 26540 explicitly.
- Impact: The page can open WebSocket connections to any loopback port.

```text
connect-src 'self' http://127.0.0.1:26540 ws://127.0.0.1:26540 ws://127.0.0.1:* ipc: http://ipc.localhost
```

### A452 Installer: DevTools flag is true on the wallpaper window in the shipped config

- Severity: medium. Type: security. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#470](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/470))
- Where: `toolchain/tauri.conf.json:14`
- Problem: The wallpaper window configuration sets devtools to true.
- Impact: DevTools may be reachable in the installed product. Whether release builds enable it is unverified.

```text
"devtools": true,
```

### A453 Scheduled tasks: autostart logon trigger has no user scope

- Severity: low. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#471](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/471))
- Where: `scheduler-tasks/UniDTKTask.xml:12`
- Problem: The LogonTrigger element has no UserId, so it applies to every account.
- Impact: Each user who logs on to the machine starts the shell in that user's session.

```text
<LogonTrigger>
<Enabled>true</Enabled>
<Delay>PT0S</Delay>
```

### A454 Scheduled tasks: elevated helper tasks set no WorkingDirectory

- Severity: low. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#472](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/472))
- Where: `scheduler-tasks/UniDTKSingboxTask.xml:34`, `scheduler-tasks/UniDTKVpnStopTask.xml:33`
- Problem: Both helper Exec actions have no WorkingDirectory, unlike the autostart task.
- Impact: The helpers run without a set working directory.

```text
<Command>@INSTALL_DIR@\vpn-helper.exe</Command>
```

### A455 Installer: a failed PATH step skips the autostart task registration step

- Severity: low. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#473](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/473))
- Where: `toolchain/windows/hooks.nsh:37`, `toolchain/windows/hooks.nsh:40`
- Problem: A nonzero PATH result jumps to the end of the hook before tasks register all runs.
- Impact: The install ends with error code 11 and no autostart task is registered.

```text
StrCmp $0 "0" uni_path_ok 0
Goto uni_hooks_done
```

### A456 Build: .cargo/config.toml begins with a byte order mark before its first line

- Severity: low. Type: bug. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#474](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/474))
- Where: `.cargo/config.toml:1`
- Problem: The file starts with a UTF-8 byte order mark before the first comment character.
- Impact: Some TOML readers reject the file. Whether cargo does here is unverified.

```text
# Linker override: rust-lld instead of MSVC link.exe (not guaranteed on PATH).
```

### A457 Installer: npm-style .tgz archives and an unreferenced package/ DLL are tracked

- Severity: low. Type: architecture. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#475](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/475))
- Where: `toolchain/tauri.conf.json:153`
- Problem: Three .tgz archives and a package/ directory holding a 28,977,664-byte tdjson-x64.dll are tracked. tauri.conf.json bundles only win32-x64/tdjson.dll.
- Impact: The repository carries a second TDLib DLL and archives whose use is unverified.

```text
"../opt/tdlib-native/win32-x64/tdjson.dll": "tdjson.dll",
```

### A458 Build: DirectML.dll is bundled only by the installer config, not staged for dev builds

- Severity: low. Type: architecture. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#476](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/476))
- Where: `toolchain/build.rs:180`, `toolchain/tauri.conf.json:163`
- Problem: The build script's native directory list omits opt/directml. No module manifest mentions DirectML.
- Impact: target/release may lack DirectML.dll in dev runs. Whether the runtime needs it there is unverified.

```text
"../opt/directml/DirectML.dll": "DirectML.dll"
```

### A459 Installer: stale gitignore entry and duplicate libmpv DLL under opt/libmpv

- Severity: low. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#477](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/477))
- Where: `.gitignore:30`, `.gitignore:23`
- Problem: The gitignore names opt/vpn-helper/*.exe, but that directory does not exist. opt/libmpv/extracted holds a second 120,323,072-byte libmpv-2.dll and mpv-dev.7z.
- Impact: The ignore list refers to a missing directory, and extra copies of large files stay on disk.

```text
opt/vpn-helper/*.exe
```

### A460 Installer: version 0.1.0 is written twice in the Tauri config and toolchain manifest

- Severity: low. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#478](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/478))
- Where: `toolchain/tauri.conf.json:3`, `toolchain/Cargo.toml:3`
- Problem: The product version is a literal in tauri.conf.json and in toolchain/Cargo.toml.
- Impact: A version change has to be made in both files.

```text
"version": "0.1.0",
```

### A461 Scheduled tasks: autostart description and template comments are in Russian

- Severity: low. Type: documentation. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#479](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/479))
- Where: `scheduler-tasks/UniDTKTask.xml:9`, `scheduler-tasks/UniDTKTask.xml:3`, `scheduler-tasks/UniDTKSingboxTask.xml:3`, `scheduler-tasks/UniDTKVpnStopTask.xml:3`, `opt/singbox/README.md:32`
- Problem: The autostart task description and the comments of the three task templates are in Russian. opt/singbox/README.md has Russian table cells.
- Impact: Task Scheduler shows the Russian description to the user, and the English-only documentation rule is broken.
- Rule: `docs.english-low-level` (docs/reference/README.md)

```text
<Description>Запускает UniDTK максимально быстро при входе в систему</Description>
```

## Stylesheets

### A462 Styles: micrographics location button uses --bg-color, a token defined nowhere

- Severity: high. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#480](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/480)) Fixed in `31b3912`.
- Where: `modules/micrographics/ui/micrographics.css:60`
- Problem: The --bg-color token is not defined anywhere in the repository. The fallback #111 is a fixed near-black background, and the text color is inherited from the card.
- Impact: On the Sea Glass preset the inherited ink is dark, so the edit-mode location button text is unreadable on the near-black box.
- Rule: `theme.tokens-only` (docs/reference/desk.md)

```text
.micrographics-card .mg-location-request { display: none; position: absolute; left: 0; bottom: -32px; height: 26px; padding: 2px 8px; background: var(--bg-color, #111); color: inherit; border: 1px solid currentColor; font: 13px var(--font-mono,Consolas,monospace); cursor: pointer; pointer-events: auto; }
```

### A463 Styles: bg-elev token is white on both presets, so borders and fills vanish on Sea Glass

- Severity: high. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#481](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/481))
- Where: `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.css:12`, `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:2949`, `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.css:338`, `core/ui/theme.js:173`
- Problem: The bg-elev-rgb token is 255, 255, 255 on both presets. It is used in 397 rgba(var(--bg-elev-rgb), a) declarations across the slice for hairlines, borders, focus cues and fills.
- Impact: On the light Sea Glass surface these borders, hairlines and focus cues are white on near-white and almost invisible.
- Rule: `theme.per-theme-colors` (docs/reference/desk.md)

```text
  border: 1px solid rgba(var(--bg-elev-rgb), 0.08);
```

### A464 Styles: desk shortcut label hardcodes #f0f0f0 and ignores the theme foreground

- Severity: high. Type: hardcode. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#482](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/482))
- Where: `core/ui/styles.css:683`
- Problem: The shortcut item text color is a fixed near-white value that bypasses the fg-rgb token. Whether it is visible depends on the desk background, which was not measured.
- Impact: On a light desk background or on the Sea Glass preset the shortcut label is hard to see.
- Rule: `theme.tokens-only` (docs/reference/desk.md)

```text
  cursor: pointer;
  color: #f0f0f0;
```

### A465 Styles: mp3 popup is fixed at 320x330 and 380x536, larger than its 250x250 card

- Severity: high. Type: customization. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#483](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/483))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/ui/mp3-player.css:808`, `modules/mp3-player/ui/mp3-player.css:850`, `modules/mp3-player/ui/mp3-player.css:853`, `modules/mp3-player/ui/mp3-player.css:862`
- Problem: The mp3 popup has fixed px sizes. The graph view forces 380x536 with !important, and the graph container is fixed at 350px. The default mp3 card is 250x250 in theme.js. Whether the popup clips inside its card was not verified.
- Impact: On small or zoomed screens the popup can overflow and cannot be resized.

```text
  width: 320px;
  height: 330px;
  width: 380px !important;
  height: 536px !important;
```

### A466 Styles: telegram danger menu label uses a fixed red, low contrast on Sea Glass

- Severity: high. Type: accessibility. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#484](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/484))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:1071`
- Problem: The destructive-action label uses a fixed red at alpha 0.9. The contrast ratio is about 2.2:1 on Sea Glass and about 6:1 on Classic (estimated, not measured).
- Impact: The danger action label is hard to read on the light preset.
- Rule: `theme.contrast` (docs/reference/desk.md)

```text
.tg-context-menu button.danger {
  color: rgba(255, 120, 120, 0.9);
```

### A467 Styles: telegram connection strip uses 9.6px amber text, low contrast on Sea Glass

- Severity: high. Type: accessibility. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#485](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/485))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:411`, `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:409`
- Problem: The connection-state strip text is 0.6rem, about 9.6px. Its fixed amber color has a contrast ratio of about 1.2:1 on Sea Glass and about 11:1 on Classic (estimated, not measured).
- Impact: The connecting or syncing hint is unreadable on the light preset.
- Rule: `theme.contrast` (docs/reference/desk.md)

```text
.tg-connection-state {
  grid-row: 2;
  padding: 5px 12px;
  font-size: 0.6rem;
  line-height: 1.2;
  color: rgba(255, 214, 130, 0.9);
```

### A468 Styles: explorer drop prompt uses fixed light-blue text, low contrast on Sea Glass

- Severity: high. Type: accessibility. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#486](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/486))
- Where: `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.css:1392`
- Problem: The drop prompt text uses a fixed light-blue color at alpha 0.85. The contrast ratio is about 1.5:1 on Sea Glass and about 10:1 on Classic (estimated, not measured).
- Impact: The drag-and-drop prompt is unreadable on the light preset.
- Rule: `theme.contrast` (docs/reference/desk.md)

```text
.drop-overlay .drop-text {
  font-size: 13px;
```

### A482 Styles: telegram reduced-motion spinner slowdown is overridden by the global 0.01ms rule

- Severity: medium. Type: bug. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#500](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/500))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:2669`, `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:2662`, `core/ui/styles.css:1330`
- Problem: The telegram reduced-motion rule sets animation-duration 2s without !important. The global rule in styles.css sets 0.01ms with !important in the same media query, so the 2s value never applies.
- Impact: Download spinners freeze in reduced-motion mode instead of slowing as the rule intends.
- Rule: `motion.reduced-motion` (docs/reference/motion.md)

```text
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .downloading::after,
  .tg-attachment-download.downloading,
  .tg-file-card.downloading .tg-file-icon-box::after,
  .tg-voice-card.downloading .tg-voice-play-btn::after,
  .tg-audio-card.downloading .tg-audio-play-btn::after,
  .tg-video-wrap.downloading .tg-video-download-overlay::after {
    animation-duration: 2s;
```

### A483 Styles: file-area-surface is defined in both styles.css and explorer.css

- Severity: medium. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#501](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/501))
- Where: `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.css:533`, `core/ui/styles.css:1286`
- Problem: The same selector is defined in styles.css at line 1286 without background-image and in explorer.css at line 533 with it. explorer.css loads later, so its values win.
- Impact: Changes to the file area must be made in two files, and only one definition takes effect.

```text
.file-area-surface {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  z-index: 10;
  pointer-events: none;
  overflow: hidden;
  background: var(--panel-wash, rgb(var(--bg-surface-rgb)));
  background-image: var(--jelly-sheen, none);
```

### A484 Styles: file-area-transition-layer z-index is 6 in styles.css and 8 in explorer.css

- Severity: medium. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#502](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/502))
- Where: `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.css:587`, `core/ui/styles.css:1311`, `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.css:549`
- Problem: The same selector sets z-index 6 in styles.css at line 1311 and 8 in explorer.css at line 587. explorer.css loads later, so the effective value is 8. explorer.css also sets .explorer-morph-plane to 9.
- Impact: The stacking order of the explorer transition layers depends on load order.

```text
.file-area-transition-layer {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
  z-index: 8;
```

### A486 Styles: 126 !important lines force module card overrides over other rules

- Severity: medium. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#504](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/504))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/ui/mp3-player.css:11`, `core/ui/styles.css:298`, `modules/glyph-bar/ui/glyph-bar.css:6`, `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:2311`, `modules/micrographics/ui/micrographics.css:80`, `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.css:1346`, `modules/vpn/ui/vpn.css:243`
- Problem: 126 declaration lines use !important. Per file: mp3 46, telegram 33, styles 25, glyph-bar 9, explorer 8, micrographics 4, vpn 1, menu 0, clock 0. Card overrides in several modules are forced this way.

```text
.music-widget:not(.expanded) {
  background: transparent !important;
  backdrop-filter: none !important;
  border: none !important;
  box-shadow: none !important;
```

### A487 Styles: visibility state is set by several classes on the same element

- Severity: medium. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#505](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/505))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/ui/mp3-player.css:829`, `core/ui/styles.css:1237`, `modules/mp3-player/ui/mp3-player.css:250`
- Problem: The music popup shows through .expanded, .surface-open and .surface-active. The explorer overlay uses .visible, .surface-active and .surface-open. The music context menu uses .surface-open and .surface-active.
- Impact: One visible state is encoded in several classes, so each must stay in sync.

```text
.music-popup.expanded,
.music-popup.surface-open {
  display: flex !important;
  opacity: 1;
  pointer-events: auto !important;
  transform: translate(-50%, -50%) translateZ(0) !important;
}
```

### A488 Styles: five transition all declarations in telegram and mp3

- Severity: medium. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#506](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/506))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:509`, `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:1591`, `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:3393`, `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:3530`, `modules/mp3-player/ui/mp3-player.css:211`
- Problem: Four telegram rules and one mp3 rule use transition all instead of named properties. Menu.css has none, and the brief counts there were not confirmed.
- Impact: Every property of these elements transitions, including properties that do not change.
- Rule: `motion.canonical-tokens` (docs/reference/motion.md)

```text
  transition: all var(--dur-hover) var(--ease-standard);
  transition: all var(--dur-transient) var(--ease-standard);
```

### A489 Styles: literal ms and s durations in animations and transitions bypass --dur tokens

- Severity: medium. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#507](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/507))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:1717`, `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:2117`, `modules/vpn/ui/vpn.css:258`, `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.css:1262`, `modules/mp3-player/ui/mp3-player.css:57`
- Problem: Telegram has 15 lines with literal ms values and 13 with literal s values. vpn has 1 ms and 1 s line, explorer has 1 s line, and mp3 has 1 s line. The --dur tokens exist and are not used on these lines.
- Impact: Motion timing cannot be changed globally through the token set.
- Rule: `motion.no-hardcoded-ms` (docs/reference/motion.md)

```text
  animation: tgHighlightPulse 1100ms cubic-bezier(0.3, 0.95, 0.35, 1) forwards;
  animation-delay: calc(var(--i, 0) * 22ms);
```

### A490 Styles: literal easing curves and ease keywords bypass --ease tokens

- Severity: medium. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#508](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/508))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/ui/mp3-player.css:906`, `modules/vpn/ui/vpn.css:210`, `modules/glyph-bar/ui/glyph-bar.css:56`, `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:1262`
- Problem: Lines outside :root use ease, linear or cubic-bezier literals. Per file: telegram 50, explorer 19, styles 10, mp3 9, vpn 5, glyph-bar 4, clock 1.
- Impact: Easing curves cannot follow the canonical easing tokens.
- Rule: `motion.canonical-tokens` (docs/reference/motion.md)

```text
  transition: color var(--dur-transient) ease, background var(--dur-transient) ease;
  animation: vpnPingPulse 1s ease-in-out infinite;
```

### A491 Styles: 182 literal border-radius px values bypass --r tokens

- Severity: medium. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#509](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/509))
- Where: `modules/mp3-player/ui/mp3-player.css:817`, `modules/micrographics/ui/micrographics.css:144`
- Problem: There are 182 border-radius declarations with literal px values. Per file: telegram 78, explorer 30, mp3 22, styles 22, menu 16, vpn 7, clock 3, micrographics 3, glyph-bar 1.
- Impact: Corner radii do not follow the radius tokens and theme radius settings.
- Rule: `theme.tokens-only` (docs/reference/desk.md)

```text
  border-radius: 14px;
```

### A492 Styles: literal blur() values bypass the blur token and the blur setting

- Severity: medium. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#510](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/510))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:1015`, `modules/mp3-player/ui/mp3-player.css:814`, `core/ui/styles.css:1228`, `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.css:796`
- Problem: 28 declaration lines use literal blur values. Per file: telegram 18, explorer 4, mp3 4, styles 2. styles.css derives the theme backdrop from the blur setting and sets it to none at 0, but these literals ignore that.
- Impact: Setting blur to 0 does not remove these glass blurs.
- Rule: `theme.tokens-only` (docs/reference/desk.md)

```text
  backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
  backdrop-filter: blur(12px) !important;
  backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
```

### A493 Styles: telegram overlays use z-index 10005-10020 and 2600 outside the token scale

- Severity: medium. Type: hardcode. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#511](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/511))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:1307`, `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:1451`, `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:1497`, `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:3726`
- Problem: Telegram overlays use literal z-index values 10005, 10010, 10020 and 2600. Those values are above the --z-surface-toast token of 5000. Where these overlays mount relative to #shell-portals, which is its own stacking context at token 3000, was not verified.
- Impact: These overlays may stack above toasts or other shell surfaces in ways the token scale does not describe.
- Rule: `theme.tokens-only` (docs/reference/desk.md)

```text
  z-index: 10005;
  z-index: 10010;
  z-index: 10020;
#shell-portals > .tg-avatar-flight { position: fixed; inset: 0; z-index: 2600; pointer-events: none; }
```

### A501 Styles: telegram input focus cue is a 0.18-alpha border, near-invisible on Sea Glass

```text
  border-color: rgba(var(--bg-elev-rgb), 0.18);
```

### A502 Styles: explorer search focus cue is a 0.16-alpha border, near-invisible on Sea Glass

- Severity: medium. Type: accessibility. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#520](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/520))
- Where: `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.css:338`, `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.css:328`
- Problem: The explorer search input removes its outline and relies on a 0.16-alpha border as the focus cue. The estimated contrast on Sea Glass is near 1:1 (estimated, not measured).
- Impact: Keyboard focus in the explorer search box is barely visible on Sea Glass.
- Rule: `theme.contrast` (docs/reference/desk.md)

```text
.search-box input:focus {
  border-color: rgba(var(--bg-elev-rgb), 0.16);
```

### A503 Styles: glyph-bar LED buttons remove outline with no focus rule

- Severity: medium. Type: accessibility. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#521](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/521))
- Where: `modules/glyph-bar/ui/glyph-bar.css:54`
- Problem: The .gb-seg LED buttons set outline none. glyph-bar.css has no focus rule for them.
- Impact: Keyboard focus on the volume LEDs is invisible.

```text
  appearance: none;
  -webkit-appearance: none;
  outline: none;
```

### A504 Styles: vpn toggle hit area is 34x20px

- Severity: medium. Type: accessibility. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#522](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/522))
- Where: `modules/vpn/ui/vpn.css:55`, `modules/vpn/ui/vpn.css:56`
- Problem: The toggle is 34px wide and 20px tall, below the 24px minimum.
- Impact: The VPN toggle is a small pointer target.

```text
.vpn-toggle {
  position: relative;
  display: inline-block;
  width: 34px;
  height: 20px;
```

### A505 Styles: vpn toggle off track alpha 0.22 gives about 2:1 against the surface

- Severity: medium. Type: accessibility. Status: not verified at runtime (found by reading the code, or an estimate). ([#523](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/523))
- Where: `modules/vpn/ui/vpn.css:70`
- Problem: The off-state track uses alpha 0.22. The estimated contrast against the Classic surface is about 2:1, below the 3:1 non-text target (estimated, not measured). Whether this is the only state cue was not verified.
- Impact: The off state of the VPN toggle is faint.
- Rule: `theme.contrast` (docs/reference/desk.md)

```text
.vpn-toggle-slider {
  position: absolute;
  cursor: pointer;
  inset: 0;
  background: rgba(var(--fg-rgb), 0.22);
```

### A506 Styles: explorer search input width jumps from 168px to 220px on focus

- Severity: low. Type: ux. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#524](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/524))
- Where: `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.css:319`, `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.css:340`
- Problem: The search input has a fixed width of 168px that becomes 220px on focus. The file-name input has max-width 120px.
- Impact: The explorer layout shifts when the search box receives focus.

```text
  width: 168px;
  width: 220px;
```

### A507 Styles: duplicated declarations in glyph-bar, micrographics and ease tokens

- Severity: low. Type: architecture. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#525](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/525))
- Where: `modules/glyph-bar/ui/glyph-bar.css:80`, `modules/glyph-bar/ui/glyph-bar.css:87`, `modules/micrographics/ui/micrographics.css:31`, `core/ui/styles.css:23`, `core/ui/styles.css:25`
- Problem: The .gb-seg.red.on and .gb-seg.red.muted-only rule bodies are identical. The .mg-atom selector is split across two rules. --ease-expo and --ease-expo-out are identical, and so are --ease-expo-out-rev and --ease-surface-exit.
- Impact: Duplicated values must be edited in several places.

```text
.gb-seg.red.on {
  background: rgba(255, 72, 58, 1);
  box-shadow:
    0 0 5px rgba(var(--status-error-rgb), 0.85),
    0 0 12px rgba(var(--status-error-rgb), 0.45);
```

### A508 Styles: literal z-index values 1000, 200, 100, 60 and others have no token

- Severity: low. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#526](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/526))
- Where: `core/ui/styles.css:1160`, `modules/micrographics/ui/micrographics.css:27`, `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:3411`, `core/ui/styles.css:1186`, `core/ui/styles.css:474`
- Problem: Several literal z-index values are used directly in components instead of the --z-surface tokens. The value 1000 appears in three places, and 100 equals the base token value without referencing it.
- Impact: Stacking changes must be made in several unrelated places.
- Rule: `theme.tokens-only` (docs/reference/desk.md)

```text
.widget-card.dragging {
  z-index: 1000;
```

### A509 Styles: hardcoded white glows and hairlines are invisible on the light preset

- Severity: low. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#527](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/527))
- Where: `modules/glyph-bar/ui/glyph-bar.css:51`, `modules/clock/ui/clock.css:27`, `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:1716`, `modules/explorer/ui/explorer.css:660`
- Problem: Several effects use literal rgba(255, 255, 255, a) white glows and hairlines. They do not follow a token.
- Impact: On the light Sea Glass preset these white effects do not show.
- Rule: `theme.per-theme-colors` (docs/reference/desk.md)

```text
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12);
  text-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15);
```

### A510 Styles: telegram accent-red fallback is a fixed red that ignores the user accent

- Severity: low. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#528](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/528))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:1162`, `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:1181`, `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:1289`
- Problem: --accent-red is defined only inside the jelly-only expanded telegram scope. Outside it, these uses fall back to the fixed red #d71921.
- Impact: On Classic these telegram accents stay red when the user changes the accent color.
- Rule: `theme.tokens-only` (docs/reference/desk.md)

```text
  border-left: 2px solid var(--accent-red, #d71921);
  color: var(--accent-red, #d71921);
```

### A511 Styles: glyph-bar red LED uses literal rgba(255, 72, 58, 1) instead of status token

- Severity: low. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#529](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/529))
- Where: `modules/glyph-bar/ui/glyph-bar.css:81`, `modules/glyph-bar/ui/glyph-bar.css:88`
- Problem: The active red LED uses a literal color at alpha 1 rather than the status-error token.
- Impact: The mute LED does not follow the status color settings.
- Rule: `theme.tokens-only` (docs/reference/desk.md)

```text
.gb-seg.red.on {
  background: rgba(255, 72, 58, 1);
```

### A512 Styles: telegram unread badge hardcodes #f4f4f4 and #050505 with 8.3px text

- Severity: low. Type: hardcode. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#530](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/530))
- Where: `modules/telegram/ui/telegram.css:165`
- Problem: The unread badge uses fixed colors. Its font size is 0.52rem, about 8.3px. Jelly overrides it later in the file.
- Impact: The badge colors do not follow the theme on Classic.
- Rule: `theme.tokens-only` (docs/reference/desk.md)

```text
  background: #f4f4f4;
  color: #050505;
  font-size: 0.52rem;
```

### A513 Styles: widget remove button is 22x22px

- Severity: low. Type: accessibility. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#531](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/531))
- Where: `core/ui/styles.css:1085`, `core/ui/styles.css:1086`
- Problem: The .widget-instance-remove button is 22x22px, below 24x24.
- Impact: The remove button is a slightly small pointer target.

```text
  z-index: 60;
  width: 22px;
  height: 22px;
```

### A514 Styles: reduced-motion coverage relies only on the global rule in styles.css

- Severity: low. Type: accessibility. Status: confirmed by reading the code. ([#532](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/532))
- Where: `core/ui/styles.css:1330`, `core/ui/styles.css:1326`
- Problem: menu, mp3, vpn, clock and glyph-bar have no own prefers-reduced-motion query. The global rule uses a literal 0.01ms value with !important.
- Impact: Those modules are only reduced-motion safe while styles.css is loaded.
- Rule: `motion.reduced-motion` (docs/reference/motion.md)

```text
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  *,
  *::before,
  *::after {
    animation-duration: 0.01ms !important;
    animation-iteration-count: 1 !important;
    transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;
```

## Documentation

### A515 Docs: dev boards and the menu audit describe a system that no longer exists

- Severity: low. Type: documentation. Status: reproduced on a live build. ([#533](https://github.com/avariiiiiiiiii/UniDTK/issues/533))
- Where: `docs/dev/menu-audit.md:1`, `docs/dev/kanban.md:1`, `docs/dev/architecture-kanban.md:1`, `docs/dev/logging.md:56`
- Problem: menu-audit.md describes a hamburger menu with tabs such as "Widgets and desktop" and "Appearance" and about 70 problems; the live menu has two tabs (Стол, Вид) after the session 131 redesign. kanban.md and architecture-kanban.md still list renderer.js as 7763 lines, Lua/WASM modules and xray as open items. docs/dev/logging.md says a frontend log level is not implemented.
- Impact: Readers and agents use stale facts as current state, against the project's docs.one-place rule.
- Rule: `docs.one-place` (docs/reference/README.md)
